Method: ECP包含三个阶段：1) Event-Triggered Causal Sampling（事件触发因果采样，锚定运动信息丰富的关键帧）；2) Event-guided Motion Saliency Filtering（事件引导运动显著性过滤，抑制事件不活跃的视觉token）；3) Event-Attention Ranking Fusion（事件注意力排序融合，用运动显著动态校准空间注意力）。

Result: 在80%视觉token缩减下，ECP超越全token基线（37.62% vs. 36.31%），实现1.89倍推理加速和52%的GFLOPs降低；在首个真实世界RGB-事件第一人称空间推理基准ESR-Real上，比全token基线提升2.68个百分点。

Conclusion: ECP是首个利用事件相机连续运动先验进行训练无关token剪枝的方法，有效兼顾了效率与性能，为第一人称动态空间推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: First-person dynamic spatial reasoning requires models to track continuous motion and precise geometric structure, but the quadratic attention cost of Transformer-based Video-LLMs makes dense visual tokens computationally expensive. Existing token pruning paradigms predominantly rely on discrete static snapshots, failing to preserve the motion and geometric cues essential for reasoning. We propose Event Cascade Pruning (ECP), to our knowledge the first training-free framework that leverages the high-frequency motion cues from event cameras as a continuous event-guided motion prior to guide token selection. ECP combines three stages: Event-Triggered Causal Sampling to anchor motion-informative keyframes, Event-guided Motion Saliency Filtering to suppress event-inactive visual tokens, and Event-Attention Ranking Fusion to calibrate spatial attention with motion-salient dynamics. With 80% visual token reduction, ECP outperforms the full-token baseline (37.62% vs. 36.31%) while achieving 1.89x inference speedup and 52% GFLOPs reduction. We further introduce ESR-Real, the first real-world RGB-event benchmark for first-person spatial reasoning, where ECP improves accuracy by 2.68 percentage points over full-token baselines.

</details>


### [38] [Return of Frustratingly Easy Unsupervised Video Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19510)
*Pengfei Wei,Yiqun Sun,Zhiqiang Xu,Yiping Ke,Lawrence B. Hsieh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种简单但有效的无监督视频域自适应方法MetaTrans，通过时间-静态减法模块分别处理跨域视频的空间和时间差异，在多个跨域动作识别任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督视频域自适应(UVDA)是一个实用但未被充分探索的问题，现有方法复杂度高且效果有限。

Method: MetaTrans采用仅包含两个基本损失项的简洁学习目标，通过时间-静态减法模块分别处理跨域视频的空间和时间差异性。

Result: 在多种跨域动作识别任务上，绝对适应性能大幅提升，相对性能增益显著优于现有UVDA基线方法。

Conclusion: 简单的架构设计可以有效地解决视频域自适应中的空间和时间差异问题，实现优异的跨域迁移性能。

Abstract: Unsupervised video domain adaptation (UVDA) is a practical but under-explored problem. In this paper, we propose a frustratingly easy UVDA method, called MetaTrans. Specifically, MetaTrans adopts a concise learning objective that contains only two fundamental loss terms. Despite the simplicity of the learning objective, MetaTrans embodies an advanced UVDA idea, that is, handling the spatial and temporal divergence of cross-domain videos separately, through a subtle model architecture design. By implementing a temporal-static subtraction module, MetaTrans effectively removes spatial and temporal divergence. Extensive empirical evaluations, particularly on various cross-domain action recognition tasks, show substantial absolute adaptation performance enhancement and significantly superior relative performance gain compared with state-of-the-art UVDA baselines.

</details>


### [39] [Are Watermarked Images Editable? SafeMark for Watermark-Preserving Text-Guided Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19511)
*Xiaodong Wu,Qi Li,Xiangman Li,Zelin Zhang,Lingshuang Liu,Jianbing Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SafeMark框架，通过在扩散编辑器的训练目标中加入阈值化的水印解码损失，实现了在保留水印完整性的同时进行文本引导的图像编辑，证明了语义可编辑性与水印完整性在根本上是兼容的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索水印图像在编辑后是否仍能保持水印完整性这一尚未充分研究的问题，旨在实现可信的图像溯源。

Method: 在扩散编辑器的训练目标中添加阈值化水印解码损失，微调解器使其在生成语义有效编辑的同时保留嵌入水印，基于信息论原理保证高比特精度与互信息下界。

Result: 在多个数据集、文本编辑方法和后编辑失真场景下，SafeMark实现了高水印比特精度和高质量语义编辑，且对常见后编辑失真具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 语义可编辑性与水印完整性在根本上是兼容的，SafeMark为生成编辑管道中的可信图像溯源提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates a fundamental yet underexplored question: can watermarked images remain editable without compromising watermark integrity? We propose SafeMark, a framework for watermark-preserving text-guided image manipulation that explicitly integrates watermark integrity into the editing process. Specifically, SafeMark adds a thresholded watermark-decoding loss directly to the diffusion editor's training objective, fine-tuning the editor so that semantically valid edits also preserve the embedded watermark at the final output. This design admits a clean information-theoretic justification: maintaining high bit-accuracy on the edited image lower-bounds the mutual information that the editor channel preserves between watermark and edited output, the quantity that fundamentally controls watermark recoverability. SafeMark is compatible with differentiable diffusion-based editors, and requires no architectural modification. Extensive evaluations across multiple datasets, text-guided editing methods, and post-edit distortion settings demonstrate that SafeMark achieves high watermark bit accuracy across diverse editing settings while maintaining high-quality semantic edits, without sacrificing robustness to common post-edit distortions. These results demonstrate that semantic editability and watermark integrity are fundamentally compatible, enabling trustworthy image provenance in generative editing pipelines.

</details>


### [40] [Dual-Prompt CLIP with Hybrid Visual Encoders for Occluded Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19527)
*Zhangjian Ji,Shaotong Qiao,Kai Feng,Wei Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DPL-ReID模型，通过双提示学习、真实世界遮挡增强和加权门控特征融合，在遮挡行人重识别任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于预训练视觉语言模型的行人重识别方法只关注增强提示特征学习，忽略了遮挡物的语义信息，导致遮挡场景下匹配困难。

Method: 提出DPL-ReID模型，包含三个核心模块：1) 双提示学习(Dual-PL)策略，利用文本提示捕获完整行人语义并保持对遮挡的鲁棒性；2) 真实世界遮挡增强(RWOA)方法，模拟真实遮挡场景；3) 加权门控特征融合(WGFF)方法，结合LSNet捕获全局信息并作为特征门控机制，引导CLIP视觉编码器生成更全面的特征表示。

Result: 在多个基准遮挡ReID数据集上取得SOTA性能。

Conclusion: DPL-ReID通过融合双提示学习、真实遮挡增强和门控特征融合，有效解决了遮挡行人重识别中的跨视图匹配问题。

Abstract: Occluded person re-identification focuses on matching partially visible pedestrians across multiple camera views. However, occlusions disrupt body-region cues, thereby complicating cross-view matching. Most person ReID methods built on pretrained vision-language models only focus on enhancing prompt-based feature learning while ignoring the semantic information of occluders. Based on the success of CLIP-ReID, we propose a novel Dual Prompt Learning ReID (DPL-ReID) model for occluded person ReID. It incorporates a Dual Prompt Learning (Dual-PL) strategy, which can utilize textual cues to capture complete pedestrian semantics and keep robustness against occlusion, and a Real-World Occlusion Augmentation (RWOA) method that realistically simulates occlusion scenarios encountered in real word to enrich occluded samples. In addition, we also design a Weighted Gated Feature Fusion (WGFF) method, which in corporates LSNet to capture global information and act as a feature-gating mechanism. This mechanism can effectively guide the CLIP visual encoder toward generating more comprehensive feature representations. Extensive experiments on several benchmark occluded ReID datasets show that our proposed DPL-ReID achieves the state-of-the art performance. The occlusion instance library are available at https://github.com/stone-qiao/DPL-ReID.

</details>


### [41] [Towards Camera-Robust 3D Localization: Equation-Anchored Tool-Use for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19528)
*Xueying Jiang,Wenhao Li,Quanhao Qian,Deli Zhao,Shijian Lu,Gongjie Zhang,Ran Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种方程锚定的工具使用框架，通过显式使用针孔反投影方程结合链式思维推理，将相机内参和深度信息带入MLLM的3D定位预测，显著提升了在变化相机内参下的3D目标检测和视觉定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在3D定位中受限于相机内参模糊性——相同图像在不同相机下对应不同3D场景。现有方法要么忽略相机参数过拟合到标准内参，要么从外部工具获取深度但仅作为参考线索，无法确定性传播相机信息。

Method: 提出方程锚定的工具使用框架：主动检索相机内参并采样多点度量深度，在链式推理中显式写出针孔反投影方程$\hat{X} = (u_c - c_x)\bar{Z}/f_x$，并将工具输出代入公式，最终回归9自由度边界框。

Result: 在0.5倍到1.5倍的缩放相机内参下，该方法在3D目标检测和3D视觉定位任务上均超越纯RGB基线及工具增强基线，在相机偏离训练尺度最大时提升最为显著。

Conclusion: 显式地将相机几何公式融入MLLM推理过程能有效解决相机内参模糊性问题，提升模型在多变相机条件下的3D定位鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: 3D localization in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), including 3D object detection and 3D visual grounding, is fundamentally limited by camera intrinsic ambiguity: the same image admits different 3D scenes under different cameras. Existing MLLMs either ignore camera parameters and overfit to a canonical training intrinsic, or retrieve depth and 3D cues from external tools but treat the returned values as reference cues (numerical hints that the model is free to interpret implicitly), both preventing camera information from being deterministically propagated into the prediction. We propose an equation-anchored tool-use framework that re-purposes spatial tools as formula variables. The proposed framework proactively retrieves camera intrinsics and samples multi-point metric depths, writes the pinhole back-projection equation $\hat{X} = (u_c - c_x)\bar{Z}/f_x$ explicitly in Chain-of-Thought (CoT), and substitutes tool outputs into the formula before regressing the final 9-DoF bounding box. On both 3D object detection and 3D visual grounding tasks under rescaled camera intrinsics from $0.5\times$ to $1.5\times$, our method outperforms RGB-only and tool-augmented baselines, with significant gains where the camera deviates most from the training scale. Code and data will be released.

</details>


### [42] [Boosting Text-to-Image Diffusion Models via Core Token Attention-Based Seed Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19532)
*Yunzhe Zhang,Hongfu Liu,Pengyu Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的种子选择方法ABSS，通过分析去噪早期阶段注意力动态来预测生成质量，从而筛选出优质随机种子，提升文本到图像扩散模型的生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型对初始随机种子高度敏感，不同种子会导致生成图像质量和提示对齐程度差异巨大。现有方法缺乏对种子效应的深入理解，且需要固定接受/拒绝阈值或额外微调。

Method: 提出Attention-Based Seed Selection (ABSS)，利用去噪早期步骤中交叉注意力对核心词（内容词）的注意力动态来评分和排序候选种子，保留top-k种子用于完整生成，无需微调、无需修改初始噪声。

Result: 在三个基准测试上，ABSS在Stable Diffusion变体上一致提升了文本-图像对齐和视觉质量，并通过人类偏好和自动对齐指标得到验证。

Conclusion: ABSS是一种轻量级、即插即用的推理时种子预筛选方法，可作为现有种子优化流程的附加组件带来额外收益。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models can synthesize high-quality images, yet the outcome is notoriously sensitive to the random seed: different initial seeds often yield large variations in image quality and prompt-image alignment. We revisit this "seed effect" and show that attention dynamics over prompt core tokens, the content-bearing words, measured during the first few denoising steps, strongly predict final generation quality. Building on this observation, we introduce Attention-Based Seed Selection (ABSS), a training-free, plug-and-play method that ranks seeds for a given prompt by leveraging cross-attention to core tokens during the denoising process. ABSS requires no finetuning and does not alter the initial noise; it scores and ranks all candidate seeds, keeps only the top-k for full generation, and discards the rest, without relying on a fixed accept/reject threshold. Operating purely at inference time, ABSS can serve as a lightweight pre-selection add-on for existing seed-optimization pipelines, enabling additional gains. Across three benchmarks, extensive experiments show that ABSS enables consistent improvements in text-image alignment and visual quality for Stable Diffusion variants, as corroborated by human preference and alignment metrics.

</details>


### [43] [CaptchaMind: Training CAPTCHA Solvers via Reinforcement Learning with Explicit Reasoning Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19538)
*Pengcheng Wang,Haoxiang Liu,Yang Dai,Xiangxiang Zeng,Guanhua Chen,Baotian Hu,Longyue Wang,Weihua Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了CaptchaBench基准测试集和CaptchaMind强化学习求解器，用于解决验证码识别问题，在8类任务上达到82.9%的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有验证码求解方法缺乏大规模训练数据和过程级标注，导致训练型方法缺失，无法有效应对需要多步视觉推理和交互的现代验证码。

Method: 构建了包含16,000个程序生成样本、涵盖8个任务类别的CaptchaBench基准，并提出了基于强化学习（RL）的CaptchaMind求解器，利用显式推理过程监督进行训练。

Result: CaptchaMind在8类任务上平均成功率达82.9%，真实世界实例上达71.0%，显著优于现有方法且不依赖闭源API。

Conclusion: CaptchaBench和CaptchaMind为验证码求解提供了训练基础和方法，证明了强化学习与推理过程监督在视觉推理任务中的有效性。

Abstract: CAPTCHAs are widely deployed as human verification mechanisms and frequently block intelligent agents from completing end-to-end automation in real-world web environments. Solving modern CAPTCHAs requires robust multi-step visual reasoning and interaction capabilities, yet training-based approaches have remained absent due to the lack of large-scale training data and process-level annotations. We introduce CaptchaBench, the first CAPTCHA benchmark designed to support large-scale training, comprising 16,000 programmatically generated samples across eight task categories with detailed region and process-level annotations. Systematic evaluation on CaptchaBench reveals that existing methods fail consistently on tasks requiring fine-grained visual detail capture and region-level comparison. We therefore present CaptchaMind, an RL-based solver trained with explicit reasoning process supervision, achieving 82.9% average success rate across eight tasks and 71.0% on real-world instances, substantially outperforming all existing methods without closed-source APIs.

</details>


### [44] [Trust It or Not: Evidential Uncertainty for Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction with Trust3R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19539)
*Zihao Zhu,Wenyuan Zhao,Nuo Chen,Chao Tian,Zhiwen Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Trust3R提出了一种轻量级的证据不确定性框架，用于前馈式3D重建。它通过门控残差均值细化与Normal-Inverse-Wishart证据头，生成闭合形式的多元Student-t分布来估计每点几何不确定性。实验表明，该方法在不确定性排名、风险覆盖和稀疏化方面优于现有方法，AURC降低25%，AUSE降低41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式几何基础模型预测的置信度分数缺乏概率解释，无法可靠指示预测几何在何处以及多大程度上可信。

Method: 结合门控残差均值细化（gated residual mean refinement）与Normal-Inverse-Wishart证据头（evidential head），生成闭合形式的多元Student-t分布，用于估计每点几何不确定性。

Result: 在ScanNet++上AURC降低25%，AUSE降低41%；在多个室内外基准上一致改善了风险覆盖（risk-coverage）和稀疏化（sparsification）指标，并提升了几何精度。

Conclusion: Trust3R提供了实用的、具有概率解释的可靠性信号，可用于下游几何pipeline中的不确定性加权，是一种有效的轻量级不确定性估计方法。

Abstract: Geometric foundation models hold promise for unconstrained dense geometry prediction from uncalibrated images. However, in current feed-forward designs, their predicted confidence scores are heuristic, lack probabilistic interpretation, and often fail to indicate where and how much the predicted geometry can be trusted. To address this gap, we present Trust3R, a lightweight evidential uncertainty framework for feed-forward 3D reconstruction. Trust3R combines gated residual mean refinement with a Normal-Inverse-Wishart evidential head, yielding a closed-form multivariate Student-t distribution for per-point geometric uncertainty. This design provides probabilistically grounded pointmap uncertainty estimates while adding moderate inference overhead. We evaluate on diverse indoor and outdoor benchmarks and compare against MASt3R's built-in confidence map as well as common uncertainty-aware baselines spanning single-pass heteroscedastic regression and sampling-based methods such as MC dropout and deep ensembles. Experimental results show that Trust3R consistently improves risk-coverage and sparsification, and generally improves geometric accuracy. These gains are reflected in stronger uncertainty ranking across benchmarks, with 25% lower AURC and 41% lower AUSE on ScanNet++, providing a practical reliability signal for uncertainty-aware weighting in downstream geometry pipelines. The project page and code are available at https://trust3r-z.github.io/.

</details>


### [45] [EgoCoT-Bench: Benchmarking Grounded and Verifiable Operation-Centric Chain of Thought Reasoning for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19559)
*Yang Dai,Dian Jiao,Tianwei Lin,Wenqiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了EgoCoT-Bench，一个用于第一人称视频细粒度操作推理的基准数据集，包含3,172个可验证的QA对，通过时空场景图引导生成并人工精修，实验发现多模态模型常出现答案正确但证据不一致的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有第一人称视频基准缺乏基于细粒度操作推理的评估，很少检查模型推理是否基于明确的时空证据，因此需要构建一个可验证、带逐步推理标注的细粒度基准。

Method: 基于时空场景图（STSG）引导的生成框架构建3,172个可验证QA对，覆盖351个视频、4个任务组12个子任务组，并经过人工标注者精修确保正确性和细粒度质量。

Result: 实验表明现有模型在第一人称细粒度推理上仍存在困难，许多多模态模型虽然答案正确，但其推理证据与答案不一致。

Conclusion: EgoCoT-Bench可作为第一人称视频理解中可验证推理的有效测试平台，为未来细粒度操作推理研究提供基准。

Abstract: The rapid development of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) has led to growing interest in egocentric video understanding, specifically the ability for MLLMs to recognize fine-grained hand-object interactions, track object state changes over time, and reason about manipulative processes in dynamic environments from a first-person perspective. However, existing egocentric video benchmarks suffer from \textbf{limited grounded rationale evaluation}, offering limited support for fine-grained operation-centric reasoning and rarely examining whether model rationales are grounded in explicit spatio-temporal evidence. To address this gap, we introduce \textbf{EgoCoT-Bench}, a fine-grained egocentric benchmark for grounded and verifiable operation-centric reasoning with explicit step-by-step rationale annotations. Overall, EgoCoT-Bench comprises 3,172 verifiable QA pairs over 351 egocentric videos separated into four task groups for a total of 12 sub-task groups, encompassing perception and retrospection, anticipation, and high-level reasoning. The benchmark is constructed through a spatio-temporal scene graphs (STSG) guided generation framework and is further refined by human annotators to ensure correctness, egocentric relevance and fine-grained quality. Experimental results show continuing difficulties with egocentric fine-grained reasoning and further reveal that many multimodal models produce explanations that are answer-correct, but have evidence that is inconsistent with the answer. We hope EgoCoT-Bench can serve as a useful testbed for grounded and verifiable reasoning in egocentric video understanding. Project page and supplementary materials are available at: https://dstardust.github.io/EgoCoT/.

</details>


### [46] [A novel YOLO26-MoE optimized by an LLM agent for insulator fault detection considering UAV images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19595)
*João Pedro Matos-Carvalho,Laio Oriel Seman,Stefano Frizzo Stefenon,Mohammad Khalaf Mohammad Khreasat,Gabriel Villarrubia González*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出YOLO26-MoE模型，通过集成稀疏混合专家模块优化高分辨率分支，结合LLM智能体进行超参数调优，在无人机绝缘子缺陷检测任务中达到0.9900 mAP@0.5，优于最新YOLO版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机结合深度学习的绝缘子检测虽已广泛应用，但小缺陷区域、异质故障模式、复杂背景和变光照条件等问题仍使检测面临挑战。

Method: 在YOLO26检测器的高分辨率分支中集成稀疏混合专家模块，实现自适应特征细化；通过工具增强的大语言模型智能体协调超参数优化、训练和评估。

Result: mAP@0.5达到0.9900，mAP@0.5:0.95达到0.9515，超过最新YOLO版本。

Conclusion: YOLO26-MoE为基于无人机的绝缘子故障检测提供了有效且可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: The inspection of electrical power line insulators is essential for ensuring grid reliability and preventing failures caused by damaged or degraded insulation components. In recent years, Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) combined with deep learning-based vision systems have emerged as an effective solution for automating this process. However, insulator fault detection remains challenging due to small defect regions, heterogeneous fault patterns, complex backgrounds, and varying imaging conditions. To address these challenges, this paper proposes an optimized YOLO26-MoE, a novel object detection architecture that integrates a sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) module into the high-resolution branch of the YOLO26 detector. The proposed modification enables adaptive feature refinement for subtle and diverse fault patterns while preserving the efficiency of a one-stage detection framework. Hyperparameter optimization, final training, and evaluation were coordinated through a tool-augmented Large Language Model (LLM) agent. The proposed model achieved 0.9900 mAP@0.5 and 0.9515 mAP@0.5:0.95, outperforming the latest YOLO versions. These results demonstrate that the proposed model provides an effective and reliable solution for UAV-based insulator fault detection.

</details>


### [47] [deadtrees.earth-aerial: A Multi-Resolution Aerial Image Dataset for Tree Cover and Mortality Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19605)
*Ayushi Sharma,Clemens Mosig,Lukas Drees,Salim Soltani,Janusch Vajna-Jehle,Aaron Sheppard,Belqis Ahmadi,Jonathan Schmid,Paul Neumeier,Nathan Jacobs,Jan Dirk Wegner,Teja Kattenborn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DTE-aerial-train和DTE-aerial-bench两个全球尺度的开放数据集，用于从厘米级航拍影像中联合分割树冠覆盖和树木死亡，并建立了强基准模型，在针叶林等挑战性区域取得了显著提升（F1分数从0.40提升至0.58，相对提升约45%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球森林面临火灾、病虫害等气候威胁，急需可扩展的树木覆盖和死亡监测方法，但目前缺乏全球统一的高质量标注数据集来支持从航拍影像中联合分割树冠和树木死亡。

Method: 构建了两个开放数据集：DTE-aerial-train（385K张1024x1024像素的航拍影像块，分辨率2.5-20厘米，含专家标注和审计的伪标签）和DTE-aerial-bench（25个全球分布的25个正射影像，共525个影像块，含高质量专家标注）。覆盖热带、温带、寒带和干旱区生物群落。

Result: 在基准测试集上建立了强基线，显著改善了各生物群落和尺度下的死亡分割效果，其中针叶林的F1分数从0.40提升至0.58，相对提升约45%。

Conclusion: 所发布的数据集、模型和代码将以开源许可公开发布，填补了全球尺度树冠与树木死亡联合分割领域的空白，为森林监测提供了重要基础资源。

Abstract: Forests worldwide are increasingly threatened by climate change and disturbances such as fire, pests, and pathogens, creating an urgent need for scalable monitoring of tree cover and tree mortality. Aerial imagery from drones and aircraft is a key data source for detailed and large-scale mapping of tree crowns and mortality. However, related progress is limited by the lack of globally representative, harmonized datasets for joint segmentation of tree cover and mortality. We introduce two novel, open, machine-learning-ready datasets to enable joint segmentation of tree cover and tree mortality from centimeter-scale aerial imagery for the first time at global scales. With DTE-aerial-train, we provide a training dataset comprising 385K image patches of size 1024x1024 pixels, with resolutions ranging from 2.5 to 20 cm. It includes multi-class expert-annotated and -audited pseudo-labels for tree cover and mortality. With DTE-aerial-bench, we provide a geographically balanced benchmark test set of 25 globally distributed orthoimages totaling 525 patches with high-quality expert annotations for both tree cover and mortality. Both the training and benchmark datasets span tropical, temperate, boreal, and dryland biomes and cover a wide range of forest structures and mortality patterns. Using the benchmark test set for evaluation, we establish strong reference baselines that improve mortality segmentation across all biomes and scales with significant gains in challenging regions, such as boreal forests, where the F1 score increases from 0.40 to 0.58 with around 45% relative improvement. All data, models, and code will be publicly released under permissive open-source licenses. An interactive visualization of the benchmark dataset is available at deadtrees.earth/releases/dte-aerial-bench.

</details>


### [48] [Spectral Integrated Gradients for Coarse-to-Fine Feature Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19607)
*Soyeon Kim,Seongwoo Lim,Kyowoon Lee,Jaesik Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Spectral Integrated Gradients (SIG)，通过基于SVD的积分路径替代传统直线路径，实现从粗到细的特征归因，在图像分类任务上取得更清晰、更准确的归因效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Integrated Gradients使用直线积分路径，会同时引入所有输入特征，导致梯度噪声累积，影响归因质量。

Method: 对基线到输入的差值进行奇异值分解（SVD），按照奇异值从大到小的顺序逐步激活奇异成分，构建从粗粒度到细粒度的积分路径。

Result: 在多个图像分类数据集上，SIG生成的归因图噪声更少、更清晰，定量指标优于现有基于路径的归因方法。

Conclusion: 基于SVD的粗到细积分路径能有效减少梯度噪声，提升特征归因的可解释性和质量。

Abstract: Integrated Gradients (IG) is a widely adopted feature attribution method that satisfies desirable axiomatic properties. However, the choice of integration path significantly affects the quality of attributions, and the standard straight-line path introduces all input features simultaneously, often accumulating noisy gradients along the way. To address this limitation, we propose Spectral Integrated Gradients, which constructs integration paths based on singular value decomposition (SVD) of the baseline-to-input difference. By progressively activating singular components from largest to smallest, SIG introduces global structure before fine-grained details, naturally following a coarse-to-fine progression. Through extensive evaluation across diverse image classification datasets, we demonstrate that SIG produces cleaner attribution maps with reduced noise and achieves improved quantitative performance compared to existing path-based attribution methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/leekwoon/sig/.

</details>


### [49] [White-Balance First, Adjust Later: Cross-Camera Color Constancy via Vision-Language Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19613)
*Shuwei Li,Lei Tan,Robby T. Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VLM-CC框架，利用视觉语言模型的迭代反馈机制实现跨相机颜色恒常性，通过VLM评估白平衡后的图像残留色偏并逐步修正光照估计，取代传统直接RGB回归方法，在多个数据集上达到最优的跨相机鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的颜色恒常性方法容易过拟合训练相机的颜色响应特性，导致跨相机泛化性能差，难以在不同相机拍摄的图像上保持一致性。

Method: 将颜色恒常性建模为迭代精炼过程：每轮迭代中，先用当前估计光照对图像进行白平衡并转换到伪sRGB，再用LoRA微调的VLM评估校正后图像，识别残留主色偏并提供定性反馈，该反馈映射为残差光照方向（红/绿/蓝）用于更新光照估计，直至收敛。

Result: VLM-CC在多个数据集上的跨相机颜色恒常性任务中达到最先进的鲁棒性表现，显著优于传统直接回归方法。

Conclusion: 将颜色恒常性重新定义为迭代感知反馈问题，用VLM评估替代直接RGB回归，有效提升了跨相机泛化能力。

Abstract: Color constancy aims to keep object colors consistent under varying illumination. Cross-camera generalization in color constancy remains challenging because learning-based models often overfit to the color response characteristics of the training camera, resulting in degraded performance on images captured by other cameras. We propose VLM-CC, a feedback-guided framework that formulates color constancy as an iterative refinement process. Instead of directly estimating the illuminant from raw input, VLM-CC performs iterative correction driven by vision-language model (VLM)-based evaluation. At each iteration, the image is white-balanced using the current estimate and converted to pseudo-sRGB. A lightweight LoRA-tuned VLM then assesses the corrected image, identifying the dominant residual color cast and providing qualitative feedback. This feedback is mapped to a residual illumination direction (red, green, or blue) and used to update the illuminant estimate until convergence. Our key idea is to reframe color constancy as an iterative perceptual feedback problem, leveraging VLM evaluation instead of direct RGB regression. By replacing direct RGB estimation with VLM-guided perceptual feedback, VLM-CC achieves state-of-the-art robustness in cross-camera color constancy across multiple datasets. Code will be available at https://github.com/NothingIknow/VLM-CC.

</details>


### [50] [Bézier Degradation Modeling for LiDAR-based Human Motion Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19620)
*Xiaoqi An,Lin Zhao,Jun Li,Chen Gong,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BMLiCap 提出了一种基于贝塞尔曲线的粗到细框架，通过时间压缩和渐进式运动重建模块，有效解决了LiDAR-based 3D人体运动捕捉中的输入不稳定和严重遮挡问题，在多个基准数据集上取得了最优精度和时间连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LiDAR的3D人体运动捕捉方法在处理不稳定输入和严重遮挡时，容易出现预测抖动甚至失败，亟需一种能够处理遮挡和噪声、生成时间连续且精确的人体运动重建方法。

Method: 提出BMLiCap粗到细框架：1) 利用时间可压缩贝塞尔曲线通过轨迹保留策略减少控制点，获得连贯且易学习的运动表示；2) 设计渐进式运动重建模块，包括Time-scale Motion Transformer (TMT) 预测多时间尺度运动曲线，以及Multi-level Motion Aggregator (MMA) 自适应融合多尺度曲线以恢复细节和时序连贯的姿态。

Result: 在LiDARHuman26M、FreeMotion、NoiseMotion和SLOPER4D四个主流基准上，BMLiCap在复杂场景中达到了最先进的精度和时间连续性，有效补偿了严重遮挡并减少了预测抖动。

Conclusion: BMLiCap通过贝塞尔曲线运动表示和渐进式多尺度融合机制，有效弥合了由遮挡和噪声引起的观测间隙，为LiDAR-based人体运动捕捉提供了一种鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: LiDAR-based 3D human motion capture has broad applications in fields such as autonomous driving and robotics, where accurate motion reconstruction is crucial. However, existing methods often struggle with unstable inputs and severe occlusions, leading to jittery or even failed pose predictions. To address these challenges, we propose BMLiCap, a coarse-to-fine framework that models motion using temporally compressible Bézier curves. By reducing control points through a trajectory-preserving strategy, we obtain a coherent and learning-friendly motion representation. To reconstruct human actions from LiDAR point-cloud cues, we design a progressive motion-reconstruction module. Specifically, a Time-scale Motion Transformer (TMT) is introduced to predict motion curves at multiple temporal scales, and a Multi-level Motion Aggregator (MMA) is utilized to adaptively fuse the multi-scale curves to recover detailed, temporally coherent poses, effectively bridging observation gaps caused by occlusions and noise. Across four mainstream benchmarks LiDARHuman26M, FreeMotion, NoiseMotion, and SLOPER4D, BMLiCap achieves state-of-the-art accuracy and temporal continuity in complex scenes, demonstrating its ability to compensate for severe occlusions and reduce prediction jitter.

</details>


### [51] [UniRefiner: Teaching Pre-trained ViTs to Self-Dispose Dross via Contrastive Register](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19622)
*Congpei Qiu,Zhaoyu Hu,Wei Ke,Zhuotao Tian,Yanhao Wu,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniRefiner框架，通过对预训练ViT中的三类伪影token进行分类和精炼，显著提升模型在密集预测任务中的空间表征能力，使EVA-CLIP-8B在ADE20K上mIoU提升9.4%至51.9%，超越DINOv2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ViT在大规模模型中存在伪影token（如高范数异常值）损害空间敏感任务的性能，而先前对伪影的定义过于狭隘，未能涵盖更广泛的语义不对齐问题。

Method: 提出UniRefiner通用精炼框架：首先系统分类三类空间伪影token，然后通过对比寄存器（contrast registers）实现双重目标——将图像token与过滤后的正常token对齐以保持语义，将寄存器token与检测到的伪影token对齐以捕获伪影信号。

Result: 仅需在~5k图像上微调数个epoch即可精炼不同ViT（包括EVA-CLIP-8B和InternViT-6B）。EVA-CLIP-8B在ADE20K上达到51.9% mIoU（+9.4%），超越DINOv2（49.1%）；零样本分割精度提升高达22%。

Conclusion: UniRefiner释放了现有大规模基础模型潜在的空间表征能力，为更广泛的应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: Representation learning with Vision Transformers (ViTs) has advanced rapidly, yet the utility of large-scale models in spatially sensitive tasks is hindered by spurious tokens. Prior efforts to mitigate this have been limited, often defining these artifacts narrowly, for example, as simple high-norm outliers. We argue that this scope is insufficient. For dense prediction tasks, we posit that any token failing to encode location-aligned semantics should be treated as a spurious artifact. This broader definition reveals a more complex problem, leading us to systematically categorize and characterize three fundamental types of spurious tokens that corrupt spatial representations. Based on this comprehensive diagnosis, we propose UniRefiner, a universal refinement framework that teaches pre-trained ViTs to self-dispose of these artifacts. UniRefiner uses contrastive registers to explicitly isolate and redistribute spurious tokens via a dual objective: (i) it aligns image tokens with filtered regular tokens to preserve semantics, and (ii) it aligns register tokens with detected spurious tokens to capture the spurious signals. Our method requires only a few epochs of fine-tuning on ~5k images to refine diverse ViTs, including massive models like EVA-CLIP-8B and InternViT-6B. Experiments demonstrate consistent and significant improvements: notably, the refined EVA-CLIP-8B achieves 51.9\% mIoU on ADE20K (+9.4\%), surpassing specialized vision models like DINOv2 (49.1\%), while zero-shot segmentation accuracy improves by up to 22\%. UniRefiner unlocks the latent spatial potential of existing large-scale foundation models, paving the way for their broader application.

</details>


### [52] [CADENet: Condition-Adaptive Asynchronous Dual-Stream Enhancement Network for Adverse Weather Perception in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19837)
*Sherif Khairy,Catherine M. Elias*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CADENet，一个无需训练的三线程异步增强检测系统，用于自动驾驶在恶劣天气下的目标检测。系统通过条件自适应增强（CAPE）、零样本天气分类（CLIP）和熵引导NMS（EG-NMS）实现低延迟检测，并揭示了标注完整性偏差导致的评估上限问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有增强后检测方法无法满足自动驾驶安全关键感知的硬实时要求；同时存在标注完整性偏差——人工标注无法识别增强后恢复的物体，导致增强方法的真实收益被低估。

Method: 提出三线程系统：Thread S（YOLOv11n）实现零延迟全帧率检测；Thread Q进行条件自适应增强（CAPE）并通过EG-NMS融合结果；Thread E使用CLIP零样本天气分类，新天气仅需新文本提示，无需标注数据和重训练。

Result: 在1327张DAWN图像上（YOLOv11m, IoU=0.5, conf=0.25），雪天Recall=0.0103, F1=0.0230；雨天F1=0.0038。Thread S维持约44 FPS。由于标注完整性偏差，报告F1值为真实增益的下界。

Conclusion: CADENet实现了无需训练的异步增强检测框架，满足实时性要求；提出Recall作为对标注偏差免疫的关键指标；零样本天气分类大幅降低了新场景部署成本。

Abstract: Adverse weather (rain, fog, sand, and snow) degrades camera-based object detection in autonomous vehicles. Existing enhancement-then-detect approaches stall the safety-critical perception loop, violating hard real-time requirements. Progress on this problem is also constrained by an under-recognized evaluation ceiling: ground truth annotated on degraded images cannot credit a detector that recovers objects the annotators themselves could not see, so a genuinely useful enhancement can register as a near-flat F1 gain. This paper presents CADENet (Condition-Adaptive Asynchronous Dual-stream Enhancement Network), a training-free three-thread system: Thread S (YOLOv11n) delivers detections at full frame rate with zero added latency; Thread Q applies condition-adaptive enhancement (CAPE) and fuses results via entropy-guided NMS (EG-NMS) without blocking Thread S; Thread E provides CLIP zero-shot weather classification, so new weather categories require only a new text prompt, with no labeled data and no retraining. Evaluated on 1327 DAWN images (YOLOv11m, IoU = 0.5, confidence = 0.25), CADENet achieves Recall = 0.0103 (micro), F1 = 0.0230 on snow, and F1 = 0.0038 on rain. We formalize the annotation completeness bias on DAWN-class data, so the reported F1 values are lower bounds on the true gain; recall is the annotation-gap-immune headline metric. Thread S sustains approximately 44 FPS regardless of enhancement load. No model retraining or additional sensor hardware is required.

</details>


### [53] [PrAda: Few-Shot Visual Adaptation for Text-Prompted Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19623)
*Gabriele Rosi,Fabio Cermelli,Carlo Masone,Barbara Caputo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PrAda方法，通过原型适配解决文本提示分割模型在域迁移下的分类错误问题，仅需少量目标域样本即可显著提升语义、实例和全景分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本提示分割模型在目标域（远离预训练数据）上性能下降，研究发现主要原因在于分类错误而非掩码生成质量。图像分类领域的少样本视觉适应已被广泛研究，但分割领域尚未探索。

Method: 提出PrAda（Prototype Adaptation）：冻结文本提示分割模型，学习类别特定原型，融合细粒度像素特征和Transformer高层表示，通过可学习重要性因子与原始文本预测结合，实现参数高效的域适应。

Result: 在5个基准数据集上覆盖语义、实例、全景分割任务，PrAda显著优于现有最先进方法和基线。

Conclusion: PrAda有效解决了文本提示分割模型在域迁移下的分类错误问题，在保持零样本能力的同时实现了强域适应，为少样本分割适应提供了新范式。

Abstract: Segmenting images is critical for visual understanding but demands extensive pixel-level annotations. Foundational models have enabled new paradigms for predicting new classes guided by textual prompts, without annotations from the target domain. Yet, on specialized target domains, far from the original pre-training, their performance degrades. We study the errors of existing methods under such domain-shift, finding that misclassification rather than mask generation is the main culprit. To address this, we introduce the novel problem of Few-Shot Visual Adaptation for text-prompted Segmentation. This kind of adaptation has been largely studied for image classification, but it remains unexplored for segmentation. We tackle this task with Prototype Adaptation (PrAda), a novel, parameter-efficient method that adapts a frozen text-prompted segmentation model. Our approach learns class-specific prototypes by combining fine-grained pixel features and high-level transformer representations, which are then fused with the original text-based predictions through a learned importance factor. This preserves the model's zero-shot potential while enabling strong adaptation to new domains. Experiments across semantic, instance, and panoptic segmentation on five benchmarks demonstrate that PrAda yields significant improvements over state-of-the-art and proposed baselines.

</details>


### [54] [FineBench: Benchmarking and Enhancing Vision-Language Models for Fine-grained Human Activity Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19846)
*Gueter Josmy Faure,Min-Hung Chen,Jia-Fong Yeh,Hung-Ting Su,Winston H. Hsu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FineBench（含199,420个多选题、64个长视频的人中心视频问答基准）和FineAgent（增强开源VLM的模块化框架），发现开源模型在细粒度人类动作理解上显著落后于GPT-5等闭源模型，而FineAgent可有效提升其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在通用视频理解上表现优异，但缺乏针对人中心细粒度理解的长视频、密集QA和时空定位的综合性基准，难以评估模型对人物动作、交互和物体操作的精细理解能力。

Method: 构建FineBench：64个15分钟长视频，199,420个密集标注的多选题，聚焦人物运动、交互和物体操作（含组合动作）；提出FineAgent：模块化框架，集成Localizer（定位器）和Descriptor（描述器）以增强开源VLM。

Result: 闭源模型GPT-5表现较好，但开源VLM显著落后，尤其在多人场景空间推理和细微动作区分上表现不佳；FineAgent持续提升多种开源VLM在FineBench上的性能。

Conclusion: FineBench为细粒度人中心视频理解提供了严格测试基准，FineAgent提供了实用的增强方案，揭示当前开源模型在此任务上仍有较大提升空间。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in general video understanding, yet they often struggle with the fine-grained comprehension crucial for real-world applications requiring nuanced interpretation of human actions and interactions. While some recent human-centric benchmarks evaluate aspects of model behaviour such as fairness/ethics, emotion perception, and broader human-centric metrics, they do not combine long-form videos, very dense QA coverage, and frame-level spatial/temporal grounding at scale. To bridge this gap, we introduce FineBench, a human-centric video question answering (VQA) benchmark specifically designed to assess fine-grained understanding. FineBench comprises 199,420 multiple-choice QA pairs densely annotated across 64 long-form videos (15 minutes each), focusing on detailed person movement, person interaction, and object manipulation, including compositional actions. Our extensive evaluation reveals that while proprietary models like GPT-5 achieve respectable performance, current open-source VLMs significantly underperform, struggling particularly with spatial reasoning in multi-person scenes and distinguishing subtle differences in human movements and interactions. To address these identified weaknesses, we propose FineAgent, a modular framework that enhances VLMs by leveraging a Localizer and a Descriptor. Experiments show that FineAgent consistently improves the performance of various open VLMs on FineBench. FineBench provides a rigorous testbed for future research into fine-grained human-centric video understanding, while FineAgent offers a practical approach to enhance such reasoning in current VLMs.

</details>


### [55] [Component-Aware Structure-Preserving Style Transfer for Satellite Sim2Real 6D Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19624)
*Yonglong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向卫星单目6D姿态估计的组件感知风格迁移框架，通过弱配对的真实-合成样本与掩码对齐调制，将真实域外观迁移至合成卫星图像，在保持几何标注的同时显著提升Sim2Real迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非合作卫星的单目6D姿态估计依赖带标注的训练数据，但真实卫星图像难以大规模获取精确的位姿标签和组件级掩码。合成渲染可提供精确几何标注，但其与真实观测之间的外观差距限制了直接迁移的效果。

Method: 构建弱配对的真实-合成样本，从无标签真实图像中提取部件级风格编码，通过掩码对齐的调制方式注入到合成卫星的对应区域；结合对抗训练、局部对比一致性、自正则化与边缘保持约束，确保生成图像可用于下游监督任务。

Result: 在5000张渲染图像和100张真实图像上，该方法取得FID 54.32和KID 0.048的最低分布差异；下游GDRNet的ADD通过率达0.260，AUC达0.611，优于代表性图像翻译基线。

Conclusion: 组件级外观迁移能够在保留合成数据几何标注的同时，有效提升卫星Sim2Real姿态估计性能。

Abstract: Monocular 6D pose estimation for non-cooperative satellites depends heavily on annotated training data, yet real satellite images with reliable pose labels and component-level masks are difficult to acquire at scale. Synthetic rendering can provide exact geometric annotations, but the appearance gap between rendered and real observations limits direct transfer to the real domain. This paper presents a component-aware structure-preserving style transfer framework for satellite synthetic-to-real data construction. The method builds weakly paired real--synthetic samples from calibrated real acquisition, ArUco-based camera-pose measurement, CAD rendering, and component masks. It then extracts part-wise real-domain style codes from unlabeled real images and injects them into corresponding synthetic satellite regions through mask-aligned modulation. To keep the generated images usable for downstream supervision, adversarial training is combined with local contrastive consistency, self-regularization, and edge-preserving constraints. Experiments are conducted on 5,000 rendered satellite images and 100 real images captured in a calibrated setup. The real images provide target-domain appearance references and final evaluation images, while the downstream GDRNet pose estimator is trained only on synthetic or translated synthetic images. Compared with representative image-translation baselines, the proposed method achieves the lowest image distribution discrepancy, with an FID of 54.32 and a KID of 0.048. When the translated data are used to train GDRNet in this target-domain adaptation setting, the ADD pass rate improves to 0.260 and the AUC improves to 0.611. These results indicate that component-level appearance transfer can improve satellite Sim2Real pose estimation in the considered calibrated setup while retaining simulation-derived geometric annotations.

</details>


### [56] [P2DNav: Panorama-to-Downview Reasoning for Zero-shot Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19634)
*Kai Sheng,Liuyi Wang,Haojie Dai,Jinlong Li,Yongrui Qin,Zongtao He,Chengju Liu,Qijun Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: P2DNav提出了一种层次化零样本视觉语言导航框架，通过全景到俯视图的双阶段决策、滑动窗口对话记忆和反思性重定向机制，在R2R-CE基准上取得了显著优于现有零样本方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本VLN方法依赖额外的航点预测模块，将高层方向推理与细粒度局部定位纠缠在一起，导致决策易出错且不稳定。

Method: 提出P2DNav层次化框架，包含三个核心组件：P2D（将导航决策分解为全景方向选择和俯视图局部定位两个阶段）、SDM（滑动窗口对话记忆，组织导航历史为多轮对话上下文）、RRM（反思性重定向机制，基于俯视图评估局部定位可靠性，必要时返回全景方向选择）。

Result: 在R2R-CE基准上，P2DNav相比SOTA零样本基于航点方法和无航点方法，SR分别提升146.6%和58.9%。

Conclusion: P2DNav有效证明了层次化解耦设计、滑动窗口记忆和反思性重定向机制在零样本VLN中的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-and-language navigation (VLN) requires an embodied agent to ground natural-language instructions into executable navigation actions in unseen environments. Existing zero-shot methods typically rely on additional waypoint prediction modules, which often entangle high-level directional reasoning with fine-grained local grounding, leading to error-prone and unstable decisions. In this paper, we propose P2DNav, a hierarchical framework for zero-shot vision-and-language navigation. P2DNav consists of three core components: Panorama-to-Downview (P2D), Sliding-Window Dialogue Memory (SDM), and Reflective Reorientation Mechanism (RRM). P2D explicitly decomposes navigation decision-making into two stages: panoramic direction selection and downview local grounding. It first selects the instruction-relevant direction from a 360° panorama, and then predicts a pixel-level target point from the downview RGB observation in that direction. In addition, SDM organizes navigation history as a multi-turn dialogue context and maintains recent visual observations within a sliding window to support long-horizon navigation. RRM further enables reflective reorientation by assessing the reliability of local grounding based on the downview observation and returning to panoramic direction selection when necessary. Experiments on the R2R-CE benchmark show that P2DNav achieves strong performance among zero-shot methods. In particular, compared with the state-of-the-art (SOTA) zero-shot waypoint-based and waypoint-free methods, P2DNav achieves SR gains of 146.6% and 58.9%, respectively, demonstrating the effectiveness of P2D, SDM, and RRM for zero-shot VLN. Code will be released for public use.

</details>


### [57] [Rethinking Visual Attribution for Chest X-ray Reasoning in Large Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20158)
*Guangzhi Xiong,Qiao Jin,Sanchit Sinha,Zhiyong Lu,Aidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出因果评估框架验证医学LVLM的视觉归因方法，发现现有方法常无法定位模型真正使用的视觉证据，并提出了基于概念归因的MedFocus方法，通过非平衡最优传输定位临床解剖区域并测量因果效应，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学LVLM在临床应用中缺乏可信的视觉证据归因能力，现有归因方法是否真正反映模型决策依据未经验证，因为缺乏内部推理过程的标准答案。

Method: 构建因果评估框架，利用反事实编辑筛选出专家标注区域因果决定模型预测的样本。提出MedFocus方法，通过非平衡最优传输定位临床相关解剖区域，并通过针对性干预测量其对模型输出的因果效应，生成空间、概念和token三级归因。

Result: 在11种归因方法、6个开源LVLM和两种输出模式上评估，发现现有归因方法常无法正确识别LVLM使用的证据。MedFocus在各项指标上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 现有视觉归因方法在医学LVLM上不可靠，MedFocus通过概念级因果归因提升了归因的可信度，向更可信的医学LVLM可解释性迈进了重要一步。

Abstract: Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs) show promise in medical applications, but their inability to faithfully ground responses in visual evidence raises serious concerns about clinical trustworthiness. While visual attribution methods are widely used to explain LVLM predictions, whether these explanations actually reflect the visual evidence underlying the model's decision is largely unverified, since ground-truth annotations for internal model reasoning are typically unavailable. We address this question for chest X-ray (CXR) reasoning by developing a causal evaluation framework that retains only CXR-VQA samples for which the expert-annotated region is verified, via counterfactual editing, to be causally responsible for the model's prediction. Using this framework across 11 attribution methods, six open-source LVLMs, and two output modes (direct answer and step-by-step reasoning), we find that existing attribution methods often fail to identify the evidence used by LVLMs. To address this failure, we propose MedFocus, a concept-based attribution method that localizes clinically meaningful anatomical regions via unbalanced optimal transport and measures their causal effect on model outputs through targeted interventions. MedFocus produces spatial, concept-level, and token-level attributions and substantially outperforms prior methods, taking a step toward more trustworthy attribution for medical LVLMs. Our data and code are available at https://github.com/gzxiong/medfocus/.

</details>


### [58] [Benchmarking and Evolving Reason-Reflect-Rectify for Reflective Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19639)
*Junjie Wang,Xinghua Lou,Jason Li,Ye Tian,Keyu Chen,Yulin Li,Bin Kang,Jacky Mai,Yanwei Li,Zhuotao Tian,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了R^3-Bench基准测试集和R^3-Refiner框架，用于评估和提升多模态模型在多轮反思式视觉生成（RVG）中的迭代推理与修正能力，实验表明该方法在多项指标上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图模型和统一多模态模型采用单次生成范式，难以处理需要迭代优化的复杂提示词，缺乏多轮反思式视觉生成能力。

Method: 形式化了Reason-Reflect-Rectify（R^3）循环框架；构建了包含600多个专家标注实例的R^3-Bench基准；提出R^3-Refiner双阶段框架，结合Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）和Hierarchical Reward Mechanism（HRM）来对齐修正与反思推理。

Result: 在R^3-Bench上，反思判决分数提升12.0%，修正分数提升9.0%；在GenEval++和T2I-CompBench上，增强了不同T2I模型的生成质量。

Conclusion: R^3-Refiner可无缝集成多种多模态大语言模型，有效提升文生图模型的生成质量，弥补了现有模型在可执行修正指令生成方面的不足。

Abstract: Text-to-Image (T2I) models and Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) have achieved remarkable progress in visual generation. However, their reliance on a single-pass generation paradigm limits their ability to handle complex prompts requiring iterative refinement. To enable multi-round Reflective Visual Generation (RVG), we formalize the Reason-Reflect-Rectify (R^3) loop as a core framework and introduce R^3-Bench, a benchmark of over 600 expert-annotated instances that quantifies iterative reasoning and rectification capabilities. Evaluation on R^3-Bench reveals a critical gap: while state-of-the-art models can identify generation errors, they fail to generate actionable rectification instructions. To bridge this gap, we propose R^3-Refiner, a dual-stage framework leveraging Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) and a Hierarchical Reward Mechanism (HRM) to better align rectification with reflective reasoning. Experiments show that R^3-Refiner achieves significant improvements on R^3-Bench (+12.0% in Reflective Verdict Score, +9.0% in Rectification Score), and can be seamlessly integrated with various MLLMs to enhance the generation quality of different T2I models on GenEval++ and T2I-CompBench. Code is available at https://github.com/xiaomoguhz/R3-Bench.

</details>


### [59] [Cross-View Splatter: Feed-Forward View Synthesis with Georeferenced Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19656)
*Matias Turkulainen,Akshay Krishnan,Filippo Aleotti,Mohamed Sayed,Guillermo Garcia-Hernando,Juho Kannala,Arno Solin,Gabriel Brostow,Daniyar Turmukhambetov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Cross-View Splatter是一种前馈方法，通过融合地面照片和卫星图像预测像素对齐的高斯点云，用于户外场景的新视角合成，相比仅使用地面图像能显著提升场景覆盖率和重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面图像采集耗时且难以大规模覆盖大型户外场景，而卫星图像易于通过公共API获取但缺乏细节。作者希望利用卫星图像的全局几何先验来弥补地面图像的不足，实现高效、高质量的户外场景三维重建。

Method: 提出Cross-View Splatter方法：将正射校正的卫星视图与GPS标记的地面照片进行融合，在统一的3D坐标框架中预测高斯点云。通过对齐地面和鸟瞰视角的特征表示，实现跨视角信息融合。

Result: 在精心策划的地理参考数据集和配对的卫星-地形数据上训练，并在新视角合成基准测试中评估，相比现有最先进方法，场景覆盖率和新视角合成质量均得到提升。

Conclusion: 融合卫星与地面图像能有效改善户外场景的三维重建效果，代码和数据将开源以促进后续研究。

Abstract: We present Cross-View Splatter, a feed-forward method that predicts pixel-aligned Gaussian splats for outdoor scenes captured at ground level AND by satellite. Faithful reconstructions require good camera coverage, but ground imagery is time-consuming and hard to capture at scale for large outdoor scenes. Fortunately, satellite imagery can provide a global geometric prior that is easy to access via public APIs. Cross-View Splatter fuses orthorectified satellite views with GPS-tagged ground photos to predict Gaussian splats in a unified 3D coordinate frame. By aligning ground and bird's-eye feature representations, our model improves scene coverage and novel-view synthesis, compared to ground imagery alone. We train on curated georeferenced datasets and paired satellite-terrain data, mined from open mapping services. We evaluate our method on a new benchmark for novel-view synthesis with georeferenced imagery allowing comparison to prior state-of-the-art methods. Our code and data preparation will be available at https://nianticspatial.github.io/cross-view-splatter/.

</details>


### [60] [WBCAtt+: Fine-Grained Pixel-Level Morphological Annotations for White Blood Cell Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19692)
*Satoshi Tsutsui,Winnie Pang,Shuting He,Bihan Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了WBCAtt+数据集，包含11种形态属性和5种像素级细胞组分的密集标注，并基于该数据集构建了属性识别和语义分割的基线模型，同时设计了考虑细胞组成结构的属性识别模型，最后展示了可解释AI等多种应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有白细胞图像数据集仅标注细胞类别，缺乏病理学家用于解释细胞判读所需的详细形态学特征，难以支持更深入的研究和可解释AI应用。

Method: 构建WBCAtt+数据集（113k图像级标签+10k分割图），提供11种形态属性和5种像素级细胞组分标注；设计融入细胞组成结构的属性识别模型。

Result: WBCAtt+是首个提供白细胞图像全面标注的数据集，基于该数据集建立了属性识别和语义分割的基线模型，属性识别模型通过融入细胞组成结构提升了识别性能。

Conclusion: WBCAtt+数据集及基线模型可支持白细胞图像的可解释AI研究（如反事实样本生成），数据集和代码已公开。

Abstract: The microscopic examination of white blood cells (WBCs) plays a fundamental role in pathology and is essential for diagnosing blood disorders such as leukemia and anemia. To support further research on WBC images, multiple datasets have been proposed. However, they mainly annotate cell categories, and lack detailed morphological characteristics that pathologists use to explain their interpretations of cells. To address this gap, we introduce WBCAtt+, a novel dataset of WBC images densely annotated with 11 morphological attributes and five pixel-level cell components. With 113k image-level labels and 10k segmentation maps, WBCAtt+ is the first to provide comprehensive annotations for WBC images. Leveraging this dataset, we provide baseline models for attribute recognition and semantic segmentation. We also design an attribute recognition model to incorporate compositional structure of cells, further improving the recognition performance. Lastly, we showcase various applications enabled by our dataset, such as explainable AI models, including counterfactual example generation. \revision{The dataset and code are publicly available\footnote{https://doi.org/10.57967/hf/8143}}.

</details>


### [61] [Physics-in-the-Loop: A Hybrid Agentic Architecture for Validated CAD Engineering Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19717)
*Elias Berger,Muhammad Usama,Jan Mehlstäubl,Bernhard Saske,Kristin Paetzold-Byhain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种混合智能体-物理架构，将基于知识的工程工具嵌入AI决策循环，通过显式物理验证实现CAD设计的闭环序列决策，在结构复杂度和编译率上取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM能生成CAD设计，但缺乏物理理解，难以用于可靠的工程设计。作者试图解决LLM隐式学习物理规律不可靠的问题。

Method: 提出Hybrid Agentic-Physical Architecture，将经过验证的基于知识的工程工具直接嵌入自主AI智能体的决策循环中，将工程设计建模为闭环序列决策过程，通过显式物理验证引导迭代生成与修改。

Result: 结构复杂度提升4.2倍，编译率提升3.5%（相比同类智能体方法），能生成更复杂且经过物理验证的设计。

Conclusion: 显式嵌入物理知识工具比隐式学习更有效，混合架构能提升生成CAD的功能有效性和结构复杂度。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate Computer-Aided Design (CAD), yet lack physical comprehension required for reliable engineering design. Instead of attempting to implicitly learn physical laws from data, we propose a Hybrid Agentic-Physical Architecture that embeds validated knowledge-based engineering tools directly into the decision making loop of autonomous AI agents. In this framework, engineering design is formulated as a closed-loop, sequential decision making process guided by explicit physical verification. Based on a load case, dedicated agents iteratively plan, generate, evaluate, and revise engineering designs using knowledge-based tools as a feedback signal. We introduce a benchmark dataset and metrics for assessing functional validity in generative CAD. Our system generates more complex and physically verified designs, with a 4.2 increase in structural complexity and improving compile rate by 3.5% compared to similar agentic methods. The codebase, prompts and dataset will be made publicly available to support reproducibility and future research.

</details>


### [62] [Efficient Long-Context Modeling in Diffusion Language Models via Block Approximate Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19726)
*Wenhu Zhang,Yiming Wu,Huanyu Wang,Yaoyang Liu,Huanzhang Dou,Senqiao Yang,Sitong Wu,Hanbin Zhao,Jiaya Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BA-Att框架，通过块级预下采样操作在紧凑空间中识别信息区域，避免依赖脆弱的先验位置模式，在50%稀疏度下保持接近全注意力的性能，注意力计算速度比FlashAttention快6.95倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型在全局连贯、双向和可控文本生成方面具有优势，但扩展到超长序列计算成本高昂。现有块稀疏注意力方法依赖固定采样模式（如尾部区域或反对角线条纹），这种先验驱动采样容易遗漏重要令牌，且在分布偏移下不稳定。

Method: 提出BA-Att框架，包含块级预下采样操作在下采样紧凑空间中识别信息区域；定义后下采样注意力映射的oracle并形式化预/后下采样方案的近似误差；引入轻量级范数排序模块和协方差补偿校正，利用对角QK方差近似全协方差以降低计算复杂度。

Result: 注意力计算速度比FlashAttention快6.95倍；在50%稀疏度下，在语言模型、多模态语言模型和视频生成模型上保持接近全注意力的性能；展现出强效率和泛化能力。

Conclusion: BA-Att通过预下采样策略有效解决了现有方法依赖先验位置模式的问题，在显著加速的同时保持了模型性能，且适用于多种模型架构，是一种高效且通用的稀疏注意力方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) enable globally coherent, bidirectional, and controllable text generation, offering advantages over traditional autoregressive LLMs, while scaling to ultra-long sequences remains costly. Many existing block-sparse attention methods select blocks by fixed sampling patterns over the high-resolution attention space, such as tail regions or anti-diagonal stripes. Such prior-driven sampling can miss salient tokens and introduce instability under distribution shifts. In this paper, we propose the Block Approximate Sparse Attention framework (BA-Att) with block-wise pre-downsampled operation, which identifies informative regions within a compact downsampled space, avoiding reliance on brittle positional priors. To analyze its theoretical behavior, we define an oracle post-downsample attention map and formalize the approximation error between pre- and post-downsample schemes. Based on this insight, we introduce a lightweight norm-sorting module and a covariance-compensated correction that approximates full covariance using diagonal QK variances, reducing computational complexity. Extensive experiments show that our operator achieves up to 6.95x acceleration over FlashAttention in attention computation, and maintains near full-attention performance at 50% sparsity across language models, multimodal language models, and video generation models, demonstrating strong efficiency and generalization.

</details>


### [63] [Tango3D: Towards Alignment for Global and Local 2D-3D Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19727)
*Zebin He,Mingxin Yang,Shuhui Yang,Hanxiao Sun,Xintong Han,Chunchao Guo,Wenhan Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Tango3D是一种统一了密集点对应和全局检索的3D基础模型，通过几何感知的2D视觉骨干和3D VAE将图像和点云映射到共享空间，实现了像素到点的局部对齐和全局语义对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D基础模型仅将点云与CLIP等冻结的视觉-语言空间进行全局对齐，无法建立细粒度的像素到点对应关系，缺乏密集对齐能力。

Method: 使用几何感知的2D视觉骨干和预训练3D VAE分别编码图像为2D块、点云为3D令牌，映射到共享空间实现局部像素-点对齐和全局语义对齐，并采用三阶段渐进式训练策略稳定联合学习。

Result: 成功实现了对象级像素到点对齐，同时保持了具有竞争力的全局检索性能，这是现有3D基础模型无法同时具备的能力。

Conclusion: Tango3D为纯几何的3D令牌注入了丰富语义，为密集3D下游任务铺平了道路。

Abstract: Existing 3D foundation models typically align point clouds to frozen vision-language spaces like CLIP, which achieve strong cross-modal retrieval by compressing 3D shape into a global vector. However, this global-only alignment cannot establish fine-grained pixel-to-point correspondence. To solve this, we present Tango3D, a foundation model that unifies dense correspondence and global retrieval. We use a geometry-aware 2D visual backbone and a pretrained 3D VAE to encode images into 2D patches and point clouds into 3D tokens. These are mapped into a single shared space to achieve both local pixel-to-point alignment and global semantic alignment. To stabilize the joint learning of dense and global objectives, we introduce a three-stage progressive training strategy. Experiments show our model successfully achieves object-level pixel-to-point alignment while maintaining competitive global retrieval, a joint capability not offered by existing 3D foundation models. By establishing a fine-grained alignment feature space, Tango3D injects rich semantics into purely geometric 3D tokens, paving the way for a wide range of dense 3D downstream tasks.

</details>


### [64] [Aero-World: Action-Conditioned Aerial Video Generation from Inertial Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19728)
*Abdul Mohaimen Al Radi,Kunyang Li,Yuzhang Shang,Mubarak Shah,Yu Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Aero-World方法，将预训练的图像到视频扩散模型转换为可控的航空视频生成器，通过动作标记流注入惯性控制信号，并利用冻结的物理探针提供惯性一致性监督。同时提出AeroBench基准评估生成视频与低级动作信号的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础视频模型在具身AI中应用有限，因为主要基于自然语言训练而非低级控制信号。对于航空飞行，运动发生在无约束的6自由度空间，微小自运动误差会导致巨大轨迹漂移，因此需要能跟随细粒度惯性动作的航空视频生成方法。

Method: 将平移加速度和角速度序列通过动作标记流注入预训练的潜在扩散变换器；使用冻结的潜在空间物理探针（在真实视频-IMU对上独立训练）在LoRA微调期间提供可微的惯性一致性监督。

Result: 在AeroBench上，Aero-World将平均AAS从57.7提升至63.6，相比AirScape，FVD更低（596.5 vs 1058.6），SSIM更高（0.595 vs 0.505），Flow-IMU相关性更高（0.44 vs 0.20）。

Conclusion: 冻结的物理探针监督是将预训练视频生成器适配至更符合动作的航空运动的一种实用机制。

Abstract: Foundation video models produce visually impressive results, but their use in embodied AI remains limited because they are primarily trained on natural language rather than low-level control signals. This limitation is especially pronounced for aerial flight, where motion occurs in unconstrained 6-DoF space and small errors in ego-motion can produce large trajectory drift. Generating aerial videos that follow fine-grained inertial actions can support scalable training and evaluation of aerial agents by providing a controllable proxy for real-world or expensive simulation data. To address this problem, we propose \textbf{Aero-World}, a method for converting a pretrained image-to-video diffusion model into a controllable aerial video generator. Aero-World injects sequences of translational acceleration and angular velocity into a pretrained latent diffusion transformer through an action-token stream. A frozen latent-space Physics Probe, trained independently on real video--IMU pairs, provides differentiable inertial-consistency supervision during LoRA finetuning while avoiding computationally expensive video decoding. We further propose \textbf{AeroBench}, a benchmark for evaluating whether generated drone videos adhere to low-level action signals. AeroBench uses Action Alignment Score (AAS) to measure agreement with commanded inertial actions and Physical Consistency Rate (PCR) to measure temporal motion stability. On AeroBench, Aero-World improves mean AAS from 57.7 to 63.6 over action-only finetuning and gives a stronger quality-control trade-off than AirScape, with lower FVD (596.5 vs. 1058.6), higher SSIM (0.595 vs. 0.505), and higher Flow-IMU correlation (0.44 vs. 0.20). These results suggest that frozen Physics Probe supervision is a practical mechanism for adapting pretrained video generators toward more action-aligned aerial motion.

</details>


### [65] [GeoMamba: A Geometry-driven MambaVision Framework and Dataset for Fine-grained Optical-SAR Object Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19734)
*Tiantong Fang,Xiuwei Wang,Jing Xiao,Wujie Zhou,Liang Liao,Mi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoMamba，一种面向光学-SAR遥感图像跨模态细粒度检索的几何驱动框架，通过几何特征注入、几何一致性约束和深度监督策略，在非对齐条件下显著提升检索性能，并在自主构建的FGOS-as数据集上达到了63.3% mAP和77.0% Rank-1准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多源遥感虽能互补观测地物，但光学与SAR图像之间存在显著的模态差异、散斑噪声和结构不一致问题，现有方法依赖配对或空间对齐样本，难以在非对齐条件下实现鲁棒的跨模态细粒度检索。

Method: GeoMamba框架包含三个核心模块：1）几何特征注入（GFI）模块，增强跨模态特征交互并引入结构先验；2）几何一致性约束（GCC）模块，结合深度监督（DS）策略，利用经典算子施加层次化几何约束以保留目标结构信息。

Result: 在自主构建的FGOS-as数据集（含11个航空航天与海洋类别）上，全对全检索设置下mAP达63.3%，Rank-1准确率达77.0%，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: GeoMamba通过几何驱动的特征学习有效解决了光学-SAR跨模态检索中的模态差异与结构不一致问题，为非对齐遥感图像检索提供了新思路。

Abstract: Multi-source remote sensing enables complementary observation of ground objects, while cross-modal fine-grained object retrieval remains challenging, especially under unaligned optical and SAR conditions. Unlike conventional retrieval settings that rely on paired or spatially aligned samples, practical optical-SAR retrieval is affected by substantial modality discrepancy, speckle noise, and structural inconsistency, which limit robust cross-modal representation learning. To address this problem, we propose GeoMamba, a geometry-driven framework tailored for optical-SAR fine-grained retrieval. Specifically, GeoMamba introduces a Geometric Feature Injection (GFI) module that enhances cross-modal feature interaction and incorporates structural priors, thereby improving the robustness of SAR representations and promoting geometry-consistent feature learning. In addition, a Geometric Consistency Constraint (GCC) module, together with a Deep Supervision (DS) strategy, imposes hierarchical geometric constraints using classical operators, which helps preserve informative object structures during representation learning. We further construct a new dataset, FGOS-as, containing 11 aerospace and maritime categories for evaluating unaligned cross-modal fine-grained object retrieval in realistic remote sensing scenarios. Extensive experiments on FGOS-as demonstrate that GeoMamba outperforms existing methods, achieving 63.3% mAP and 77.0% Rank-1 accuracy in all-to-all retrieval setting.

</details>


### [66] [FlowErase-RL: Rethinking Concept Erasure as Reward Optimization in Flow Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19739)
*Yi Sun,Zhiqi Zhang,Xinhao Zhong,Yimin Zhou,Shuoyang Sun,Bin Chen,Shu-Tao Xia,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了FlowErase-RL，首个基于GRPO框架的流匹配模型概念擦除方法，通过动态双路径奖励机制优化目标概念抑制与生成保真度，在多项任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配模型在提升文本到图像生成质量的同时带来了安全风险，现有概念擦除方法（推理时干预效果有限，SFT依赖精确对齐数据且难以扩展）存在局限性，亟需更高效、可扩展的概念擦除方法。

Method: 提出FlowErase-RL框架，将概念擦除建模为奖励优化问题；设计动态双路径奖励机制，包含概念擦除（CE）奖励（抑制目标概念）和非目标空间（NS）奖励（保持生成保真度），并通过性能驱动的切换策略自适应平衡两条路径。

Result: 在色情内容、物体和艺术风格擦除任务上达到SOTA擦除性能，同时保持强图像质量和语义对齐，对对抗攻击具有鲁棒性，可扩展到多概念场景。

Conclusion: FlowErase-RL为流匹配模型中的安全可控生成建立了新范式，证明了基于强化学习的方法在概念擦除任务上的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: Recent advances in flow matching models have significantly improved text-to-image generation quality, but also introduce growing safety risks due to the generation of harmful or undesirable content. Existing concept erasure methods are either inference-time interventions with limited effectiveness or rely on supervised fine-tuning (SFT), which requires precisely aligned data and struggles with scalability and multi-concept settings. In this paper, we propose \emph{FlowErase-RL}, the first GRPO-based framework for concept erasure in flow matching models. We reformulate concept erasure as a reward optimization problem and introduce a \textbf{dynamic dual-path reward mechanism} that jointly optimizes (i) a Concept Erasure (CE) reward to suppress target concepts and (ii) a Non-target Space (NS) reward to preserve generative fidelity. The two reward paths are adaptively balanced during training via a performance-driven switching strategy, enabling stable optimization without explicit supervision. Extensive experiments on nudity, object, and artistic style erasure demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art erasure performance while maintaining strong image quality and semantic alignment. Moreover, it exhibits robust resistance to adversarial attacks and scales effectively to multi-concept scenarios. Our results establish a new paradigm for safe and controllable generation in flow matching models.

</details>


### [67] [Real-World On-Vehicle Evaluation of Embedding-Based Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19744)
*Albert Schotschneider,Daniel Bogdoll,Svetlana Pavlitska,Ahmed Abouelazm,Johann Marius Zoellner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于预训练视觉Transformer嵌入和最近邻相似度的实时异常检测方法，仅需单张参考图像即可建模常态，无需监督训练，在道路异常检测基准上表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通场景异常检测对自动驾驶安全至关重要，但收集代表性异常数据困难。现有方法高度专业化且依赖Cityscapes抽象语义类定义的常态，难以适应多样化真实场景。

Method: 利用预训练视觉Transformer嵌入作为基础模型，通过隐语义特征空间中的最近邻相似度检测偏离。基于逐块处理生成密集异常掩码，仅需单张参考图像即可鲁棒地建模常态，无需显式监督和数据集特定训练。

Result: 在Road Anomaly基准测试上取得良好性能，并在真实自动驾驶车辆上表现出一致的定性行为，成功突出不同场景中的语义异常物体。

Conclusion: 简单的、基于参考的方法能够在现实操作条件下提供有用的异常信号，表明该方法适合实际部署。

Abstract: Detecting anomalies in traffic scenes is crucial for ensuring safety in autonomous driving, yet collecting representative anomalous data remains challenging. Existing anomaly detection methods are highly specialized and rely on normality as defined by the abstract semantic Cityscapes classes, making it difficult to adapt to diverse real-world scenarios. We propose an adaptable real-time anomaly detection method that leverages foundation models in the form of pretrained vision transformer embeddings to detect deviations via nearest-neighbor similarity in the latent semantic feature space. Based on patch-wise processing, the algorithm produces dense anomaly masks, allowing for the localization of detected anomalies. The method robustly models normality through a single reference image. This formulation avoids explicit supervision and dataset-specific training, making it suitable for real-world deployment. We evaluate the method on standard benchmarks and on an automated vehicle in real-world scenarios. Despite its simplicity, the method achieves good performance on the Road Anomaly benchmark and demonstrates consistent qualitative behavior in practice, successfully highlighting semantically unusual objects in diverse scenes. These results suggest that simple, reference-based methods can provide useful anomaly signals under realistic operating conditions.

</details>


### [68] [CPC-VAR:Continual Personalized and Compositional Generation in Visual Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19750)
*Junhao Li,Xinhao Zhong,Yi sun,Yuxia Qiao,Bin Chen,Shu-Tao Xia,Yaowei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究VAR模型中的持续个性化生成，提出统一框架解决顺序学习中的灾难性遗忘和多概念合成中的特征纠缠问题，通过梯度概念神经元选择（GCNS）和上下文感知组合策略实现高效持续个性化生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VAR模型个性化方法局限于静态场景，无法适应不断变化的用户需求：顺序概念学习导致严重灾难性遗忘，多概念合成存在特征纠缠和属性不一致问题。

Method: 提出统一框架包含两个核心组件：1）基于梯度的概念神经元选择（GCNS），识别概念相关神经元并仅约束任务间冲突参数以缓解遗忘；2）上下文感知组合策略，通过多分支特征建模和空间条件引导的局部交叉注意力融合实现精准解耦的概念组合。

Result: 在长序列持续个性化任务中显著提升性能，在多概念图像合成方面优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文方法展示了VAR模型在可扩展和可控个性化生成方面的潜力。

Abstract: Visual autoregressive (VAR) models have recently emerged as an efficient paradigm for text-to-image generation. Despite their strong generative capability, existing VAR-based personalization methods remain limited to static settings, failing to accommodate evolving user demands. In particular, sequential concept learning leads to severe catastrophic forgetting, while multi-concept synthesis often suffers from feature entanglement and attribute inconsistency. In this work, we present the first systematic study of continual personalized generation in VAR models. We identify two key challenges: (i) preserving previously learned concepts during sequential customization, and (ii) composing multiple personalized concepts in a controllable manner. To address these issues, we propose a unified framework with two core components. For continual single-concept learning, we introduce Gradient-based Concept Neuron Selection (GCNS), which identifies concept-relevant neurons and constrains only conflicting parameters across tasks, effectively mitigating forgetting without additional model expansion. For multi-concept synthesis, we propose a context-aware composition strategy that performs multi-branch feature modeling and localized cross-attention fusion guided by spatial conditions, enabling precise and disentangled concept composition. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly improves performance in long-sequence continual personalization while achieving superior results in multi-concept image synthesis compared to existing baselines. These findings highlight the potential of VAR models for scalable and controllable personalized generation.

</details>


### [69] [Preferences Order, Ratings Anchor: From Fused Expert Aesthetic Ground Truth to Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19776)
*Yuanpei Zhao,Jie Lin,Chao Zhang,Yilin Wang,Mao Li,Chenhui Li,Jie Hou,Tangjie Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个匹配双协议（成对偏好+点式评分）的图像美学评估基准PPaint，覆盖中国画五个美学维度；并提出PSDistill方法，将VLM成对判断通过Elo机制转化为伪评分，实现单次推理的高效美学评分器，性能超越现有开源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IAA基准仅采用单一标注协议（成对偏好或点式评分），两者在受控条件下的互补性尚未被量化衡量。

Method: 构建PPaint基准（15位专家、150幅中国画、双协议、五维度；共45,900个成对判断）；提出PSDistill方法，利用Elo参考池将VLM成对偏好转化为校准伪评分，并通过置信度加权排序优化训练VLM为单次评分器。

Result: 在单个画类上训练的Qwen3-VL-8B，平均SRCC从0.504提升至0.709（跨三类），超越所有开源基线（包括专用模型ArtiMuse），与闭源Gemini-3.1-Pro差距在0.04 SRCC内，推理成本仅为单次前向。

Conclusion: 成对偏好与点式评分具有互补优势，融合两者可提升美学评估准确性；PSDistill方法实现了无需标注数据的VLM美学评分能力，且具备跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Pairwise preferences and pointwise ratings are the two dominant annotation protocols in image aesthetic assessment (IAA), yet existing benchmarks adopt only one, leaving their complementarity unmeasured under controlled conditions. We introduce PPaint, a matched dual-protocol benchmark in which 15 domain experts, 5 per category, annotate 150 Chinese paintings under both protocols across five aesthetic dimensions, collecting 45,900 pairwise expert judgments through a locally dense preference design alongside the matched ratings. The matched design reveals complementary strengths: preferences yield more consistent ordinal rankings, while ratings anchor the absolute score scale. Fusing both signals via two independent preference-to-score methods yields a fused expert ground truth on which the two constructions converge to nearly identical scores. The same preference-to-score principle extends to label-free VLM training. PSDistill converts VLM pairwise judgments into calibrated pseudo-scores via an Elo reference pool, and trains the same VLM with confidence-weighted ranking optimization to produce a single-pass aesthetic scorer. Trained on a single painting category, the distilled Qwen3-VL-8B improves mean SRCC from 0.504 to 0.709 across all three categories, outperforming all open-source baselines including the dedicated aesthetic model ArtiMuse and matching closed-source Gemini-3.1-Pro within 0.04 SRCC at single-pass inference cost, with cross-domain transfer further validated on APDDv2. We will release the full PPaint dataset and training code.

</details>


### [70] [Fast 4D Mesh Generation by Spatio-Temporal Attention Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19786)
*Dvir Samuel,Yuval Atzmon,Gal Chechik,Yoni Kasten*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的4D网格生成加速方法，通过时空注意力链传播信息，在9秒内生成4D网格，实现13倍加速，并支持更长的视频序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D网格生成方法速度慢、计算成本高，难以扩展到长视频序列，需要更高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出时空注意力链（Spatio-Temporal Attention Chain）框架，通过锚网格顶点到潜在token的映射，沿着时间对应关系传播信息，再通过潜在到顶点的注意力恢复帧特定顶点，无需显式的匹配计算。

Result: 9秒生成4D网格，实现13倍加速；支持长达16倍的原视频长度而不降低质量；在2D目标追踪和4D追踪上取得零样本竞争性能；首次实现可靠的相机估计。

Conclusion: 该方法在显著加速的同时提升了时间对应质量，并解锁了先前方法不支持的新能力（相机估计），为4D网格生成的实用化迈出重要一步。

Abstract: 4D mesh generation has recently emerged as a powerful paradigm for recovering dynamic 3D structure from videos, but existing methods remain slow, computationally expensive, and difficult to scale to longer sequences. We introduce a training-free approach that accelerates 4D mesh generation while improving temporal correspondence quality. Our key observation is that temporal correspondences emerge inside a 4D backbone long before its generated meshes become visually accurate. We exploit this with a general framework we call Spatio-Temporal Attention Chain which propagates information across space and time. Starting from vertices on an anchor mesh, the chain maps vertices to latent tokens. It then follows temporal correspondences in latent space, and recovers frame-specific vertices through latent-to-vertex attention. This design avoids expensive explicit matching while preserving anchor mesh details and thereby improving dynamic mesh geometry and temporal consistency.
  Compared to state-of-the-art, our method generates a 4D mesh in 9 seconds, achieving a $13\times$ speedup while producing higher-quality results. Moreover, our approach scales to videos up to $16\times$ longer without degrading mesh quality. Beyond generation, the improved correspondences enable competitive zero-shot performance on two downstream tasks: 2D object tracking and 4D tracking. We further show that our framework enables reliable camera estimation, a capability not supported by prior 4D mesh generation methods.

</details>


### [71] [Mechanisms of Object Localization in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19792)
*Timothy Schaumlöffel,Martina G. Vilas,Gemma Roig*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过机械可解释性工具分析了两类视觉语言模型（LLaVA-1.5和InternVL-3.5）中的目标定位机制，发现定位依赖一种"容器化"机制：对齐目标的token定义空间范围，而语义排列对边界框预测影响很小。定位和分类由少数注意力头介导，且大部分头是各自专用的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在基本分类和定位任务上表现不佳，分类机制已有较多研究，但目标定位过程尚不明确，因此本文旨在揭示定位的底层计算机制。

Method: 使用token消融、注意力头删除和因果中介分析等机械可解释性工具，对LLaVA-1.5和InternVL-3.5两个代表性模型族进行系统性分析。

Result: 定位依赖容器化机制；少数注意力头介导因果效应；LLaVA中位于早-中层，InternVL中位于中-后期层；分类和定位共享早期处理但依赖不同的专用头。

Conclusion: 首次提供了VLM中定位的层和头级别解释，揭示了狭窄的计算通路，可指导未来模型设计和目标定位目标的优化。

Abstract: Visually-grounded language models (VLMs) are highly effective in linking visual and textual information, yet they often struggle with basic classification and localization tasks. While classification mechanisms have been studied more extensively, the processes that support object localization remain poorly understood. In this work, we investigate two representative families, LLaVA-1.5 and InternVL-3.5, using a suite of mechanistic interpretability tools, including token ablations, attention knockout, and causal mediation analysis. We find that localization is driven by a containerization mechanism in which object-aligned tokens define the spatial extent of the object, while the semantic arrangement of tokens within those boundaries is largely irrelevant to the predicted box. Only a very small set of attention heads mediates the causal effect for both classification and localization, concentrating in early-mid layers for LLaVA and mid-late layers for InternVL. The two tasks share some early processing but ultimately depend on largely distinct specialized heads. Overall, we provide the first layer- and head-level account of localization in VLMs, revealing narrow computational pathways that can guide future model design and grounding objectives.

</details>


### [72] [When Preference Labels Fall Short: Aligning Diffusion Models from Real Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19839)
*Weiyan Chen,Weijian Deng,Yao Xiao,Weijie Tu,ZiYi Dong,Ibrahim Radwan,Liang Lin,Pengxu Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探讨用真实数据替代人工标注偏好对来对齐扩散模型，通过对比真实图像与生成/扰动样本构建偏好信号，取得了与现有偏好对齐方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏好对齐方法依赖模型生成的偏好对进行监督，这种相对监督在样本质量均不佳时会产生歧义，难以区分真正理想的输出。

Method: 采用数据为中心的视角，将真实图像作为参考点，通过与生成或扰动样本进行对比来构造偏好信号，无需人工标注的偏好对。

Result: 基于真实数据的监督能够有效指导扩散模型对齐，性能与现有基于偏好的方法相当。

Conclusion: 真实数据为偏好对齐提供了实用且互补的监督来源，为标注高效的 alignment 策略指明了方向。

Abstract: Preference alignment aims to guide generative models by learning from comparisons between preferred and non-preferred samples. In practice, most existing approaches rely on preference pairs constructed from model-generated images. Such supervision is inherently relative and can be ambiguous when both samples exhibit artifacts or limited visual quality, making it difficult to infer what constitutes a truly desirable output. In this work, we investigate whether real data can serve as an alternative source of supervision for preference alignment. We adopt a data-centric perspective and study a curation strategy that treats real images as reference points and constructs preference signals by contrasting them with generated or perturbed samples, without requiring manually annotated preference pairs. Through empirical analysis, we show that real-data-based supervision provides effective guidance for aligning diffusion models and achieves performance comparable to existing preference-based methods. Our results suggest that real data offers a practical and complementary source of supervision for preference alignment and highlight directions of label-efficient alignment strategies. Code and models are available at https://cwyxx.github.io/RealAlign.

</details>


### [73] [A Framework for Evaluating Zero-Shot Image Generation in Concept-based Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19855)
*Giacomo Astolfi,Matteo Bianchi,Riccardo Campi,Antonio De Santis,Marco Brambilla*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用零样本文本到图像生成模型为概念型XAI方法生成合成概念数据集的可行性，通过四项分析评估其与真实概念的一致性，发现当前生成模型虽提供了捷径但仍面临挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念型XAI方法依赖于大量标注图像来表示每个概念，这限制了其可扩展性。因此，作者希望利用零样本T2I生成模型作为合成概念数据集的来源，以降低对大规模人工标注的依赖。

Method: 使用预定义提示词生成概念图像，并通过四项互补分析评估其与真实概念的 faithfulness：（1）概念表示相似性对比；（2）同概念子集内相似性随规模变化；（3）下游解释任务性能；（4）从测试图像中移除概念对生成概念解释的影响。

Result: 当前T2I生成模型虽然为概念型XAI提供了快捷方式，但研究发现使用零样本管道生成的合成数据仍存在挑战，并引发了关于其在模型分析中应用的开放性问题。

Conclusion: 零样本T2I生成模型有望成为概念型XAI的便捷数据来源，但合成数据与真实数据之间的差距仍需进一步研究和改进。

Abstract: Concept-based Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) interprets deep learning models using human-understandable visual features (e.g., textures or object parts) by linking internal representations to class predictions, thereby bridging the gap between low-level image data and high-level semantics. A major challenge, however, is the reliance on large sets of labeled images to represent each concept, which limits scalability. In this work, we investigate the use of zero-shot Text-to-Image (T2I) generative models as a source of synthetic concept datasets for concept-based XAI methods. Specifically, we generate concepts using predefined prompts and evaluate their faithfulness to real ones through four complementary analyses: (1) comparing synthetic vs. real concept images via concept representation similarity; (2) evaluating their intra-similarity by comparing pairs of subsets of the same concept with progressively increasing size; (3) evaluating their performance for downstream explanation tasks using relevant class images; (4) evaluating how removing a concept from tested class images affects explanations of generated concepts. While current T2I generative models promise a shortcut to concept-based XAI, our study highlights challenges and raises open questions about the use of synthetic data generated by zero-shot pipelines in model analyses. The resulting dataset is available at https://github.com/DataSciencePolimi/ZeroShot-T2I-Concepts.

</details>


### [74] [Eyes on VLM: Benchmarking Gaze Following and Social Gaze Prediction in Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19859)
*Hengfei Wang,Anshul Gupta,Pierre Vuillecard,Jean-Marc Odobez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EyeVLM是一个系统性评估框架，用于测试视觉语言模型（VLM）在注视理解任务中的表现。研究发现，当前VLM在注视跟随（几何定位）和社交注视预测（多人物交互推理）两项核心任务上，与纯视觉模型相比仍有显著差距，即使通过微调也难以完全弥补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型已成为通用多模态推理器，但它们在理解人类注视和注意力行为方面的能力尚未被系统评估，而这是人类行为理解中的核心任务。

Method: 提出了EyeVLM评估框架，涵盖两个核心任务（注视跟随和社交注视预测）和两种评估方式（零样本测试与任务特定QA微调），并对多种开源/闭源VLM进行系统比较。

Result: 当前VLM缺乏精确的注视理解能力，在零样本和微调设置下均与专业视觉模型存在差距；标准训练有助于缩小差距，但仍需显著改进。
