Drawing on a three-year dataset of conspiracy-related posts from X, and applying computational linguistic analysis alongside survival modelling, we find that conspiracy claims with greater semantic mutations have substantially longer lifespans. Mutations in psycholinguistic properties, including pronouns, social reference words, cognitive process terms, risk- and health- related vocabularies, are associated with extended lifespans. Mutations in actor, action and target (AAT) categories are associated with longer lifespans as well. Qualitative analysis identifies two predominant mutation patterns: simplification and assimilation, at both linguistic and AAT structural levels. Taken together, the results advance our understanding of how language mutations contribute to conspiracy persistence online and shed lights on longitudinal content moderation strategies. We argue that content moderation should consider the mutability of conspiracy claims and focus on the core claims that can address their potential variations.

</details>


### [150] [PromptRad: Knowledge-Enhanced Multi-Label Prompt-Tuning for Low-Resource Radiology Report Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20052)
*Ying-Jia Lin,Tzu-Chin Lo,Ping-Chien Li,Chi-Tung Cheng,Chien-Hung Liao,Hung-Yu Kao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PromptRad是一种知识增强的提示调优方法，用于在低资源环境下进行放射学报告的多标签标注，仅需32个标注样本即可超越传统方法，并接近GPT-4的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于规则的标注器难以处理临床报告中多样化的描述，而微调预训练语言模型需要大量标注数据，这在临床环境中往往不可用。

Method: 将多标签分类重构为掩码语言建模任务，并利用UMLS元词表中的同义词构建多词动词器来丰富类别表示，仅微调PLM而不添加额外分类层。

Result: 在肝脏CT报告实验中，仅用32个标注训练样本即可超越基于字典和传统微调的基线方法，且使用更小的模型达到了与GPT-4竞争的性能。

Conclusion: PromptRad能更有效地捕捉复杂否定模式，是数据稀缺临床场景下报告标注的有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Automatic report labeling facilitates the identification of clinical findings from unstructured text and enables large-scale annotation for medical imaging research. Existing rule-based labelers struggle with the diverse descriptions in clinical reports, while fine-tuning pre-trained language models (PLMs) requires large amounts of labeled data that are often unavailable in clinical settings. In this paper, we propose PromptRad, a knowledge-enhanced multi-label \textbf{prompt}-tuning approach for \textbf{rad}iology report labeling under low-resource settings. PromptRad reformulates multi-label classification as masked language modeling and incorporates synonyms from the UMLS Metathesaurus into a multi-word verbalizer to enrich category representations. By fine-tuning the PLM without additional classification layers, PromptRad requires substantially less labeled data than conventional fine-tuning. Experiments on liver CT reports show that PromptRad outperforms dictionary-based and fine-tuning baselines with only 32 labeled training examples, and achieves competitive performance with GPT-4 despite using a much smaller model. Further analysis demonstrates that PromptRad captures complex negation patterns more effectively than existing methods, making it a promising solution for report labeling in data-scarce clinical scenarios. Our code is available at https://github.com/ila-lab/PromptRad.

</details>


### [151] [Rewarding Beliefs, Not Actions: Consistency-Guided Credit Assignment for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20061)
*Wenjie Tang,Minne Li,Sijie Huang,Liquan Xiao,Yuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ReBel算法，通过显式建模信念状态并引入信念一致性监督和信念感知分组，解决了部分可观测环境下长程交互任务的时序信用分配问题，在ALFWorld和WebShop上相较GRPO提升高达20.4%的成功率，样本效率提升2.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境中，智能体信念会随时间漂移，且延迟奖励模糊了中间决策的因果影响，导致长程交互任务中的时序信用分配困难。

Method: 提出ReBel算法：1) 显式建模结构化信念状态以总结交互历史；2) 信念一致性监督，将预测信念与观测反馈的差异转化为密集自监督信号；3) 信念感知分组，在相似信念状态下比较轨迹以得到更稳健的优势估计。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop基准上，ReBel相比GRPO任务成功率提升最高20.4个百分点，样本效率提升2.1倍。

Conclusion: 信念感知的自监督是部分可观测环境下可靠长程决策的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) is a promising paradigm for improving large language model (LLM) agents on long-horizon interactive tasks. However, in partially observable environments, incomplete observations cause agent beliefs to drift over time, while delayed rewards obscure the causal impact of intermediate decisions, exacerbating temporal credit assignment challenges. To address this, we propose ReBel (Reward Belief), a process-level reinforcement learning algorithm that explicitly models structured belief states to summarize interaction history and guide subsequent policy learning. ReBel introduces belief-consistency supervision, converting discrepancies between predicted beliefs and observed feedback into dense self-supervised signals without requiring external step-wise annotations or verifiers. It also employs belief-aware grouping to compare trajectories under similar belief states, yielding more robust and lower-variance advantage estimates. We evaluate ReBel on challenging long-horizon benchmarks, including ALFWorld and WebShop. ReBel improves task success by up to $20.4$ percentage points over the episode-level baseline GRPO and increases sample efficiency by $2.1\times$. These results suggest that belief-aware self-supervision is a promising direction for reliable long-horizon decision-making under partial observability. Code is available at: https://github.com/Fateyetian/Rebel.git.

</details>


### [152] [Text-to-SPARQL Generation with Reinforcement Learning: A GRPO-based Approach on DBLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20066)
*Jann Pfeifer,Debayan Banerjee,Ricardo Usbeck*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探索使用基于结果奖励的强化学习（GRPO）训练小型语言模型（Qwen3-1.7B）在学术领域进行零样本Text-to-SPARQL生成，无需金标准查询注释。实验表明GRPO显著优于零样本基线，但监督微调（DoRA）在相同模型规模下准确率更高。消融实验显示执行反馈奖励贡献最大，基于金查询的塑形奖励增益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱问答方法依赖大型模型或金查询标注，但金查询标注成本高、难以获取，需要探索无需完整监督的替代训练策略。

Method: 对Qwen3-1.7B模型应用组相对策略优化（GRPO），在DBLP-QuAD数据集上使用自然语言问题+实体关系符号提示，结合执行反馈、结构约束和答案级奖励进行训练，并尝试加入金查询塑形奖励的变体。

Result: GRPO大幅优于零样本基线并展现良好泛化能力；监督DoRA微调在相同规模下取得更高总体准确率；消融分析表明执行反馈奖励贡献最大，塑形奖励增益有限。

Conclusion: 基于结果的强化学习在金查询不可用、无法进行词元级监督时，是一种可行的训练策略。

Abstract: Knowledge graph question answering seeks to translate natural language questions into executable queries over knowledge graphs, but existing approaches often rely on large models or full supervision in the form of gold query annotations. This study examines whether reinforcement learning with outcome-based rewards can train a small instruction-tuned language model to perform zero-shot Text-to-SPARQL generation in the scholarly domain. Group-Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is applied to the Qwen3-1.7B model on DBLP-QuAD, using prompts that combine natural language questions with symbolic hints about entities and relations. Training relies on execution feedback, structural constraints, and answer-level rewards, with an additional variant that incorporates gold-query-based shaping. The resulting models are compared to the unmodified zero-shot baseline and to a supervised DoRA-finetuned baseline across answer-level accuracy, execution accuracy, category-wise scores, and generalization to held-out templates. GRPO substantially improves over the zero-shot baseline and exhibits competitive generalization, while supervised DoRA finetuning achieves higher overall accuracy on the same model scale. Ablation analyses indicate that execution-based rewards account for most gains, with additional shaping yielding limited additional benefit, suggesting that outcome-based reinforcement learning is a viable training strategy when gold queries are unavailable for token-level supervision.

</details>


### [153] [CopT: Contrastive On-Policy Thinking with Continuous Spaces for General and Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20075)
*Dachuan Shi,Hanlin Zhu,Xiangchi Yuan,Wanjia Zhao,Kejing Xia,Wen Xiao,Wenke Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CopT提出了一种反向的推理流程：先给出草稿答案，再基于该答案进行思考与修正，并通过对比连续嵌入与离散标记的模型支持度来评估答案可靠性，从而在不额外训练的情况下提升准确率并大幅降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的CoT将思考作为回答的前提，即使模型已经能直接给出答案，仍需执行冗长的推理过程，导致不必要的token消耗和延迟，这种现象被称为"表演性推理"。作者希望打破"先思考后回答"的固有范式。

Method: 1) 反向流程：先让模型生成草稿答案，再基于该答案进行有策略的思考（on-policy thinking）用于反思和修正。2) 将连续嵌入作为推理时的对比验证器，通过对比模型在离散token输入和连续嵌入输入下对同一生成标记的支持度，计算序列级反向KL散度估计值，用于衡量答案可靠性。3) 当答案不可靠时，通过第二个KL估计器动态控制草稿答案对后续思考的可见性，保留有用信息同时避免被误导。

Result: 在数学、代码和智能体推理任务上，CopT将峰值准确率提升了最高23%，同时在保持相当或更高准确率的情况下，将token使用量减少了最高57%。

Conclusion: CopT无需额外训练即可有效识别模型何时已具备答案，避免不必要的推理开销，同时通过对比验证机制为答案可靠性提供了可解释的理论依据（其估计值与未解析潜状态和已发射答案标记之间的互信息相关）。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) is a standard approach for eliciting reasoning capabilities from large language models (LLMs). However, the common CoT paradigm treats thinking as a prerequisite for answering, which can delay access to plausible answers and incur unnecessary token costs even when the model is able to identify an answer before extended thinking, a behavior known as performative reasoning. In this paper, we introduce CopT, a reformulated reasoning pipeline that reverses the usual order of thinking and answering. Instead of thinking before answering, CopT first elicits a draft answer and then invokes subsequent on-policy thinking conditioned on its own draft answer for reflection and correction. To assess whether the draft answer should be trusted, CopT recasts continuous embeddings as inference-time contrastive verifiers. Specifically, it contrasts the model's support for the same generated tokens under discrete-token inputs and continuous-embedding inputs, yielding a sequence-level reverse KL estimator for answer reliability. Our analysis shows that under certain assumptions, the expected estimate equals the mutual information between the unresolved latent state and the emitted answer token, explaining why it captures answer-relevant uncertainty rather than arbitrary uncertainty in the latent state. When the answer is deemed insufficiently reliable, CopT performs further on-policy thinking, where a second KL estimator dynamically controls draft-answer visibility, preserving useful partial information while reducing the risk of being misled by unreliable content. Across mathematics, coding, and agentic reasoning tasks, CopT improves peak accuracy by up to 23% and reduces token usage by up to 57% at comparable or higher accuracy, without any additional training. The code is available at https://github.com/sdc17/CopT.

</details>


### [154] [ThoughtTrace: Understanding User Thoughts in Real-World LLM Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20087)
*Chuanyang Jin,Binze Li,Haopeng Xie,Cathy Mengying Fang,Tianjian Li,Shayne Longpre,Hongxiang Gu,Maximillian Chen,Tianmin Shu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ThoughtTrace是首个大规模数据集，记录了用户在真实多轮人机对话中的自述想法（发送提示的原因及对助手回复的反应），包含1,058名用户、2,155段对话、17,058轮次和10,174条想法标注。研究发现这些想法在语义上与消息不同，前沿大模型难以从上下文推断，且能改善用户行为预测和提供细粒度对齐信号以训练个性化助手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话AI数据集仅记录用户说什么，而不记录用户想什么，缺乏对用户认知动态的理解。作者希望填补这一空白，通过收集用户自述想法来揭示人机交互背后的认知过程。

Method: 收集1,058名用户与20种语言模型进行真实多轮对话，要求用户自述每个发送提示的原因以及对助手回复的反应，最终构建包含10,174条想法标注的ThoughtTrace数据集。随后通过语义分析、大模型推理能力测试等方式验证数据的独特性和价值。

Result: ThoughtTrace捕获了长程、主题多样的交互；用户想法在语义上与消息截然不同，前沿大模型难以从对话上下文中准确推断；想法内容多样且与对话阶段相关；想法作为推理上下文能改善用户行为预测，基于想法的改写为训练个性化助手提供了细粒度对齐信号。

Conclusion: 用户想法是研究人机交互认知动态的新数据模态，有助于构建能更好理解并适应用户潜在目标、偏好和需求的个性化助手。

Abstract: Conversational AI has now reached billions of users, yet existing datasets capture only what people say, not what they think. We introduce ThoughtTrace, the first large-scale dataset that pairs real-world multi-turn human--AI conversations with users' self-reported thoughts: their reasons for sending prompts and reactions to assistant responses. ThoughtTrace comprises 1,058 users, 2,155 conversations, 17,058 turns, and 10,174 thought annotations collected across 20 language models. Our analysis shows that ThoughtTrace captures long-horizon, topically diverse interactions, and that thoughts are semantically distinct from messages, difficult for frontier LLMs to infer from context, diverse in content, and tied to conversation stages. We further demonstrate the utility of thoughts for downstream modeling. First, thoughts improve user-behavior prediction as inference-time context. Second, thought-guided rewrites provide fine-grained alignment signals for training personalized assistants. Together, ThoughtTrace establishes user thoughts as a new data modality for studying the cognitive dynamics behind human--AI interaction and provides a foundation for building assistants that better understand and adapt to users' latent goals, preferences, and needs.

</details>


### [155] [MixRea: Benchmarking Explicit-Implicit Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20128)
*Yuanqing Cai,Ziyi Huang,Minhao Liu,Lixin Duan,Wen Li,Yanru Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM存在类似人类"无意视盲"的认知缺陷——在显式指令下忽略重要隐性线索。作者提出MixRea基准（2246道题，9种推理类型）和PRCP提示方法，发现最佳模型Gemini 2.5 Pro仅42.8%一致性，验证了该现象的普遍性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受人类认知中"无意视盲"（Inattentional Blindness）理论启发，探究LLM是否因训练语料中嵌入的注意力偏差，在面对显式任务指令时忽略微妙但重要的上下文线索。

Method: 1) 提出显式-隐式推理任务，构建MixRea基准（2246道多选题，覆盖9种推理类型，不同显/隐信息分布）；2) 评估21个先进LLM；3) 提出Potential Relation Completion Prompting (PRCP)提示方法，通过恢复被忽视的因果关联来改进推理。

Result: 最佳推理模型Gemini 2.5 Pro仅达42.8%的一致性，揭示广泛的"无意视盲"现象。该局限性在多种多源推理任务中持续存在。

Conclusion: LLM存在类似人类的无意视盲认知缺陷，需要开发更具认知对齐能力的模型。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into high-stakes decision-making. Inspired by the theory of \emph{inattentional blindness} in human cognition, we investigate whether LLMs, trained on human-preferred corpora that embed attentional biases, exhibit a similar limitation: \emph{failing to attend to subtle yet important contextual cues under explicit task instructions}. To evaluate this, we introduce the task of \textbf{explicit-implicit reasoning} and present \textbf{MixRea}, a benchmark of 2,246 multiple-choice questions across 9 reasoning types with varying distributions of explicit and implicit information. Evaluation of 21 advanced LLMs shows that even the best-performing reasoning model (Gemini 2.5 Pro) achieves only 42.8\% consistency, revealing widespread inattentional blindness. To mitigate this, we propose \textbf{Potential Relation Completion Prompting (PRCP)}, a prompting method that improves reasoning by recovering overlooked causal relations. Further analysis shows that this limitation persists across diverse multi-source reasoning tasks, highlighting the need for more cognitively aligned models.

</details>


### [156] [Less Back-and-Forth: A Comparative Study of Structured Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20149)
*Saurav Ghosh,Gabriella Polach,Abdou Sow*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了结构化提示设计（特别是检查清单式提示）相比原始提示和澄清式问题提示，在提升大语言模型回答质量的同时减少用户交互成本的效果。实验表明，检查清单提示在四项任务（摘要、规划、解释、编码）和三个模型（ChatGPT、Claude、Grok）上均表现最优，平均评分7.50/8，且使用的token数最少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在开放任务中常因提示不够明确而产生低质量回答，用户需要额外交互来修正，增加了使用成本。本文旨在探索一种更高效的结构化提示设计方法，既能提升回答质量，又能减少用户不必要的交互。

Method: 对比三种提示条件：原始提示、检查清单改进提示和澄清式问题提示。在四个任务类型（摘要、规划、解释、编码）上，使用三个LLM系统（ChatGPT、Claude、Grok）进行评估，采用统一评分标准（任务完成度、正确性、合规性、清晰度）打分。

Result: 检查清单改进提示的平均评分为7.50/8，高于原始提示的5.67和澄清式问题提示的6.67。同时，检查清单提示使用的平均token数少于原始提示和澄清式问题提示，实现了最佳的质量-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 简单的检查清单式提示设计能够有效提升大语言模型的回答质量，同时减少不必要的用户交互，是一种高效且实用的提示策略。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used for open-ended tasks, but underspecified prompts can lead to low-quality answers and additional interaction. This paper studies whether structured prompt design improves response quality while reducing user effort. We compare three prompt conditions: a raw prompt, a checklist-improved prompt, and a clarifying-question prompt. We evaluate these conditions across four task types--summarization, planning, explanation, and coding--using three LLM systems: ChatGPT, Claude, and Grok. Each output is scored with a unified rubric covering task completion, correctness, compliance, and clarity. Checklist-improved prompts achieved the highest mean rubric score, 7.50 out of 8, compared with 5.67 for raw prompts and 6.67 for clarifying-question prompts. Checklist prompts also produced the best quality-effort tradeoff, using fewer average tokens than both raw and clarifying prompts. These results suggest that a simple prompt checklist can improve LLM responses while reducing unnecessary interaction.

</details>


### [157] [From Seeing to Thinking: Decoupling Perception and Reasoning Improves Post-Training of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20177)
*Juncheng Wu,Hardy Chen,Haoqin Tu,Xianfeng Tang,Freda Shi,Hui Liu,Hanqing Lu,Cihang Xie,Yuyin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉语言模型（VLM）的性能瓶颈主要在于视觉感知而非推理能力。通过将训练分解为视觉感知、视觉推理和文本推理三个阶段，并采用强化学习（RL）优化视觉感知，模型在提升推理准确率的同时显著缩短了推理路径。阶段性训练在多个视觉数学和感知任务上取得了领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM强调长链思维推理，但作者发现视觉任务的性能瓶颈实际在于视觉感知能力不足。因此，系统研究感知与推理在VLM后训练中的相互作用关系。

Method: 将VLM能力分解为三个训练阶段：视觉感知、视觉推理、文本推理，每个阶段使用专门的训练数据。采用RL（而非基于标题的SFT）优化视觉感知，并采用阶段性训练策略。

Result: 阶段性训练相比混合训练，推理准确率提升1.5%，同时推理路径缩短20.8%。在WeMath上+5.2%，RealWorldQA上+3.7%的改进。该能力维度的课程与传统的难度维度课程正交，可叠加使用。

Conclusion: 视觉感知需要通过强化学习进行针对性优化，且应作为基础在阶段性训练中优先巩固。更好的感知能力可以减少对冗长推理的依赖，实现更高效、更准确的推理。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) emphasize long chain-of-thought reasoning; yet, we find that their performance on visual tasks is primarily limited by a lack of visual perception as opposed to reasoning itself. In this work, we systematically study the interplay between perception and reasoning in VLM post-training by decomposing their capabilities into three separate training stages: visual perception, visual reasoning, and textual reasoning, incorporating specialized training data. We demonstrate that visual perception (a) requires targeted optimization with specialized data; (b) serves as a fundamental scaffold that should be solidified through staged training before refining visual reasoning; and (c) is more effectively learned via RL than caption-based SFT. Our experiments across multiple VLMs demonstrate that staged training consistently improves both visual perception and reasoning performance over merged training. Notably, models trained with our approach achieve 1.5% higher reasoning accuracy with 20.8% shorter reasoning traces, suggesting that superior perception reduces the need for excessive reasoning. Furthermore, we show that this capability-based staging represents a new curriculum dimension orthogonal to traditional difficulty-based curricula, and combining both yields further additive gains. Our staged-training models achieve superior performance among open-weight VLMs, establishing advanced results on several visual math and perception (e.g., +5.2% on WeMath and +3.7% on RealWorldQA) tasks compared with the base counterpart.

</details>


### [158] [TIDE: Efficient and Lossless MoE Diffusion LLM Inference with I/O-aware Expert Offload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.20179)
*Zhiben Chen,Youpeng Zhao,Yang Sui,Jun Wang,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TIDE是一个面向扩散大语言模型（dLLM）的资源高效推理系统，利用专家激活的时间稳定性，通过间隔刷新策略和数学规划调度，在无需训练的情况下实现最高1.5倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLM）虽在硬件利用和双向上下文上优于自回归模型，但随着MoE架构扩展，在资源受限设备上的部署面临I/O开销和计算瓶颈的挑战，现有方法无法有效解决。

Method: 利用块内扩散过程中专家激活的时间稳定性，提出基于间隔的专家刷新策略（I/O感知）；将推理调度建模为数学规划问题，求解最优间隔以最小化I/O流量和CPU计算。

Result: 在单GPU-CPU系统上，TIDE在LLaDA2.0-mini和LLaDA2.0-flash模型上分别实现了相比先前基线最高1.4倍和1.5倍的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: TIDE是一种无需模型训练的无损优化方法，为dLLM推理提供“免费午餐”式的加速效果，有效解决MoE架构dLLM在资源受限设备上的部署难题。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) have emerged as a competitive alternative to autoregressive (AR) models, offering better hardware utilization and bidirectional context through parallel block-level decoding. However, as dLLMs continue to scale up with mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures, their deployment on resource-constrained devices remains an open challenge. Existing AR-based methods often incur either prohibitive I/O overhead or significant compute bottlenecks. In this work, we propose TIDE, a novel resource-efficient inference system that leverages the temporal stability of expert activations during the diffusion process within the block. Specifically, we leverage the temporal stability of expert activations during the diffusion process within the block and introduce an interval-based expert refresh strategy that updates the expert placement in an I/O-aware fashion. To ensure optimal performance, we formulate the inference scheduling as a mathematical programming problem, solving for the optimal interval that minimizes I/O traffic and CPU computation. Most importantly, TIDE is a lossless optimization that requires no model training, providing a "free lunch" acceleration for dLLM inference. In a single GPU-CPU system, we demonstrate that TIDE achieves up to 1.4$\times$ and 1.5$\times$ throughput improvements over prior baselines on LLaDA2.0-mini and LLaDA2.0-flash models, respectively.

</details>


### [159] [ReacTOD: Bounded Neuro-Symbolic Agentic NLU for Zero-Shot Dialogue State Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19077)
*Yanjun Lin,Zimo Xiao,Kartik Natarajan,Mahesh Sankaranarayanan,Niraj Nawanit,Rakshit Parashar,Austin Zhang,Karthik Konaraddi,Rishita Mote,Wei Niu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReacTOD是一种基于受限神经符号架构的任务型对话系统，通过将NLU重构为离散工具调用并结合自纠正ReAct循环与确定性验证，在MultiWOZ和SGD基准上实现了零样本SOTA性能，显著提升了准确率和格式合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中等规模LLM在任务型对话中容易出现幻觉和格式错误，导致错误的系统动作（如酒店预订日期错误），需要一种可预测且可靠的方法来约束模型行为。

Method: 提出ReacTOD架构：(1) 将NLU重构为受限于ReAct循环的离散工具调用；(2) 符号验证器强制动作合规性、模式一致性和共指一致性；(3) 增量状态预测和按需历史检索保持提示简洁。

Result: MultiWOZ 2.1零样本SOTA：gpt-oss-20B达52.71% JGA（超此前最佳14个百分点）；Qwen3-8B达47.34%。SGD基准上Claude-Opus-4.6达80.68% JGA。错误自纠正率达93.1%，单次推理准确率提升高达9.3个百分点。

Conclusion: ReacTOD通过神经符号结合与自纠正机制，有效提升了任务型对话系统的可靠性和准确性，在参数受限模型中展现出强大的跨基准泛化能力。

Abstract: Task-oriented dialogue systems -- handling transactions, reservations, and service requests -- require predictable behavior, yet the moderately-sized LLMs needed for practical latency are prone to hallucination and format errors that cascade into incorrect actions (e.g., a hotel booked for the wrong date). We propose ReacTOD, a bounded neuro-symbolic architecture that reformulates NLU as discrete tool calls within a self-correcting ReAct loop governed by deterministic validation. A bounded ReAct loop enables iterative self-correction, improving accuracy by up to 9.3 percentage points over single-pass inference on MultiWOZ. A symbolic validator enforces action compliance, schema conformance, and coreference consistency on every dialogue state update, achieving a 93.1% self-correction rate on intercepted errors and producing structured execution traces. Incremental state prediction and on-demand history retrieval keep prompts compact, empirically improving instruction adherence in parameter-constrained models. On MultiWOZ 2.1, ReacTOD achieves a new zero-shot state-of-the-art: gpt-oss-20B reaches 52.71% joint goal accuracy, surpassing the previous best by 14 percentage points, while Qwen3-8B achieves 47.34% with only 8B parameters. On the Schema-Guided Dialogue (SGD) benchmark, ReacTOD with Claude-Opus-4.6 achieves 80.68% JGA under fully end-to-end evaluation with predicted domains, and Qwen3-32B reaches 64.09% -- demonstrating cross-benchmark generalization without task-specific training data.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [Position: Let's Develop Data Probes to Fundamentally Understand How Data Affects LLM Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18801)
*Shiqiang Wang,Herbert Woisetschläger,Hans Arno Jacobsen,Mingyue Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"数据探针"方法，通过从随机过程中生成合成序列，系统研究数据特征如何影响大语言模型在各工作流阶段（训练、微调、对齐、上下文学习等）的行为，从而建立数据与模型性能之间的理论基础，超越当前依赖大规模实验的经验启发式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对何种数据对大语言模型有用以及其原因的理解仍不清晰，现有方法依赖大规模公共数据集的经验启发式进行数据过滤和数据集构建，计算密集且缺乏理解数据特征驱动模型行为本质的原理性方法。

Method: 提出从适当定义的随机过程中生成合成序列（称为"数据探针"），这些序列具有可观察的统计特性，可通过典型集等理论概念进行描述，用于在LLM工作流的一个或多个阶段中观察模型行为。

Result: 数据探针能够系统揭示数据特征如何影响模型性能、泛化能力和鲁棒性，为数据在LLM训练和推理中的作用提供基础性见解。

Conclusion: 数据探针方法为理解数据在LLM中的作用提供了超越经验启发式的理论路径，有助于建立数据特性与模型行为之间的系统性关联。

Abstract: Data is fundamental to large language models (LLMs). However, understanding of what makes certain data useful for different stages of an LLM workflow, including training, tuning, alignment, in-context learning, etc., and why, remains an open question. Current approaches rely heavily on extensive experimentation with large public datasets to obtain empirical heuristics for data filtering and dataset construction. These approaches are compute intensive and lack a principled way of understanding the essence of how specific data characteristics drive LLM behavior. In this position paper, we advocate for the need of developing systematic methodologies for generating synthetic sequences from appropriately defined random processes, with the goal that these sequences can reveal useful characteristics when they are used in one or multiple stages of the LLM workflow. We refer to such sequences as data probes. By observing LLM behavior on data probes, researchers can systematically conduct studies on how data characteristics influence model performance, generalization, and robustness. The probing sequences exhibit statistical properties that can be viewed using theoretical concepts, such as typical sets, which are generalized to describe the behaviors of LLMs. This data-probe approach provides a pathway for uncovering foundational insights into the role of data in LLM training and inference, beyond empirical heuristics.

</details>


### [161] [Operationalizing Document AI: A Microservice Architecture for OCR and LLM Pipelines in Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18818)
*Yao Fehlis,Benjamin Bengfort,Zhangzhang Si,Vahid Eyorokon,Prema Roman,Patrick Deziel,Devon Slonaker,Steve Veldman,Ben Johnson,Joyce Rigelo,Michael Wharton,Steve Kramer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种微服务架构，用于封装文档理解中的分类、OCR和LLM字段提取等多个模型管道，并分享了在生产规模下每小时处理数千份多页文档的经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术界过度关注新模型设计，忽视了模型在工业级生产环境中的部署与运行问题，导致定义与规模化生产之间存在巨大差距。

Method: 采用微服务架构，设计混合分类策略，将GPU推理与CPU编排分离，使用异步处理IO密集型操作，并实施独立的水平扩展策略。

Result: 通过批量性能分析发现两个关键结果：OCR而非LLM解析主导端到端延迟；系统并发瓶颈由共享GPU推理容量决定，而非工作线程数量。

Conclusion: 为从业者提供了超越基准测试、可在生产环境中实际部署文档理解系统的具体架构模式。

Abstract: Academic research tends to focus on new models for document understanding creating a wide gap in the literature between model definition and running models at production scale. To close that gap, we present a microservice architecture that encapsulates pipelines of multiple models for classification, optical character recognition (OCR), and large language model structured field extraction as well as our experience running this pipeline on thousands of multi-page documents per hour. We describe our primary design decisions, including a hybrid classification, separation of GPU-bound inference from CPU-bound orchestration, use of asynchronous processing for the many IO-bound operations in the pipeline, and an independent, horizontal scaling strategy. Using batch profiling, we identified two surprising qualitative findings that shape production deployments: OCR, not language-model parsing, dominates end-to-end latency, and the system saturates at a concurrency determined by shared GPU-inference capacity rather than worker count. Our goal is to provide practitioners with concrete architectural patterns for building document understanding systems that work beyond the benchmark; effectively operationalizing models in production.

</details>


### [162] [Learn-by-Wire Training Control Governance: Bounded Autonomous Training Under Stress for Stability and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19008)
*Anis Radianis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LBW-Guard 是一种位于 AdamW 优化器之上的轻量级自治训练控制层，通过观察训练遥测并施加有界控制，显著提升了 LLM 训练的稳定性与效率，在 Qwen2.5-7B 上实现困惑度 18.7% 的改善及 1.10x 加速，尤其在激进学习率下保持可训练性，优于梯度裁剪基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型训练在激进学习率、规模扩展和运行时压力下越来越容易出现不稳定性、训练质量下降和计算资源浪费，现有方法（如梯度裁剪）无法有效解决这些问题。

Method: 提出 Learn-by-Wire Guard（LBW-Guard），一种有界自治训练控制治理层，运行于 AdamW 优化器之上。它不替换优化器更新规则，而是通过观察训练遥测数据，识别不稳定敏感区域，对优化器执行施加有界控制，同时保持固定训练目标不变。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-7B 参考设置中，困惑度从 13.21 降至 10.74（改善 18.7%），端到端训练时间从 392.54s 降至 357.02s（1.10x 加速）。在 LR=3e-3 强压力下，AdamW 困惑度退化为 1885.24，而 LBW-Guard 保持可训练（11.57）；在 LR=1e-3 下，AdamW 为 659.76，LBW-Guard 为 10.33。梯度裁剪基线无法重现该效果。

Conclusion: 稳定性敏感的 LLM 训练可以从优化器之上的治理平面中获益。LBW-Guard 证明有界运行时控制能够在压力下保持高效计算，且与优化器替换和局部梯度抑制有本质区别，为大规模训练稳定性提供了一种新的系统级解决方案。

Abstract: Modern language-model training is increasingly exposed to instability, degraded runs, and wasted compute, especially under aggressive learning-rate, scale, and runtime-stress conditions. This paper introduces Learn-by-Wire Guard (LBW-Guard), a bounded autonomous training-control governance layer that operates above AdamW. Rather than replacing the optimizer update rule, LBW-Guard observes training telemetry, interprets instability-sensitive regimes, and applies bounded control to optimizer execution while preserving fixed training objectives.
  We evaluate LBW-Guard in a Qwen2.5-centered stress-and-robustness suite using WikiText-103, with Qwen2.5-7B as the empirical anchor, model-size comparisons against Qwen2.5-3B and Qwen2.5-14B, learning-rate stress tests, gradient-clipping baselines, and a no-LoRA TinyLlama-1B full-parameter sanity check. In the 7B reference setting, LBW-Guard reduces final perplexity from 13.21 to 10.74, an 18.7% improvement, while reducing end-to-end time from 392.54s to 357.02s, a 1.10x speedup. Under stronger learning-rate stress, AdamW degrades to 1885.24 final perplexity at LR=3e-3 and 659.76 at LR=1e-3, whereas LBW-Guard remains trainable at 11.57 and 10.33, respectively. Gradient-clipping baselines do not reproduce this effect.
  These results support a scoped systems conclusion that stability-sensitive LLM training can benefit from a governance plane above the optimizer. LBW-Guard provides evidence that bounded runtime control can preserve productive compute under stress while remaining distinct from optimizer replacement and local gradient suppression.

</details>


### [163] [AgentNLQ: A General-Purpose Agent for Natural Language to SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19010)
*Olena Bogdanov,Yeunji Jung,Chandra Dhir,Pareekshitreddy Gaddam,Saurabh Jain,Lakshmi Tumati,Vijay Parthasarathy,Anup Shirgaonkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种多智能体NL2SQL方法，通过语义增强的schema表示、业务规则注入和自校正机制，在BIRD基准上达到78.1%的语义准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM能力快速发展，NL2SQL在准确率上尚未达到人类专家水平，需要进一步改进算法。

Method: 设计了一种多智能体解决方案，包含优化编排器（用于规划、编排、反思和自校正）、先进的schema丰富方法（创建上下文感知元数据），以及用户提供的业务规则。

Result: 在BIRD-SQL基准上实现了78.1%的语义准确率，并验证了跨不同领域和数据集的准确性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过多智能体协作和schema增强有效提升了NL2SQL的准确性，具有良好的泛化性。

Abstract: Natural language to SQL (NL2SQL) conversion is an important problem for researchers and enterprises due to the ubiquitous importance of relational databases in broad-ranging practical problems. Despite the rapid advancements in the capabilities of LLMs, NL2SQL has not reached parity in accuracy with human expert SQL writers, hence needing additional improvements in NL2SQL algorithms. This study presents a new multi-agent method for NL2SQL that achieves 78.1% semantic accuracy on the BIg Bench for LaRge-scale Database (BIRD) benchmark. Our method leverages a semantically enriched representation of user-provided schema, adds user-provided business rules, and produces accurate SQL queries. The main contributions of this study are (a) We designed an optimized new orchestrator in a multi-agent solution that uses LLMs to plan, orchestrate, reflect, and self-correct to generate accurate SQL queries, (b) We developed an advanced schema enrichment method that creates context-aware metadata to improve accuracy, and (c) We demonstrated the accuracy and generalizability of the method across different domains and datasets by evaluating it on the BIRD-SQL benchmark.

</details>


### [164] [DecisionBench: A Benchmark for Emergent Delegation in Long-Horizon Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19099)
*Yuxuan Gao,Megan Wang,Yi Ling Yu,Zijian Carl Ma,Ao Qu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DecisionBench是一个用于评估长周期智能体工作流中委托（delegation）能力的基准平台，包含固定任务套件、模型池、委托接口和多元评估指标。通过对11个模型、7个供应商族、23375个任务实例的参考扫描，发现：1）不同感知条件下的端任务质量无显著差异；2）路由保真度在7.5%-29.5%之间，传递渠道主导内容；3）完美委托的对比天花板显示存在15-31个百分点的未开发空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准缺乏对长周期智能体工作流中委托行为（即一个模型调用另一个模型完成任务）的系统评估方法，特别是缺乏对路由质量、成本、延迟等多维度的标准化评估框架。

Method: 构建了一个包含GAIA、tau-bench、BFCL multi-turn任务套件，11个模型（7个供应商族）的基准平台，设计了call_model接口和可选的read_profile通道，采用确定性技能注释层，并定义多轴指标（质量、成本、延迟、委托率、路由保真度-at-k、供应商自偏好、反事实委托天花板）。

Result: 三个核心发现：1）四个感知条件下的端任务平均质量统计上无差异（|beta|<=0.010, p>=0.21）；2）在平均质量相近的情况下，路由保真度-at-1范围从7.5%到29.5%，且传递渠道（按需工具 vs 预加载描述）主导描述内容；3）完美委托的对比天花板显示每个套件都存在15-31个百分点的未开发空间。

Conclusion: DecisionBench为评估智能体委托能力提供了标准化基础，揭示了仅关注质量会遗漏委托信号，并指出当前方法存在显著提升空间，为未来的路由学习、记忆增强、自适应配置生成等方向提供了评估框架。

Abstract: We introduce DecisionBench, a benchmark substrate for emergent delegation in long-horizon agentic workflows. The substrate fixes a task suite (GAIA, tau-bench, BFCL multi-turn), a peer-model pool (11 models, 7 vendor families), a delegation interface (call_model plus an optional read_profile channel), a deterministic skill-annotation layer, and a multi-axis metric suite covering quality, cost, latency, delegation rate, routing fidelity-at-k, vendor self-preference, and a counterfactual-delegation ceiling. The substrate is agnostic to how peer information is generated or delivered, so learned routers, richer peer memories, adaptive profile construction, and multi-step delegation can all be evaluated against it. We characterize the substrate with a five-condition reference sweep on the full pool (n=23,375 task instances). Three benchmark-level findings emerge: (i) mean end-task quality is statistically indistinguishable across the four awareness conditions (|beta| <= 0.010, p >= 0.21), so quality-only evaluation would miss the orchestration signal; (ii) routing fidelity-at-1 ranges from 7.5% to 29.5% across conditions at near-equal mean quality, with delivery channel (on-demand tool vs. preloaded description) dominating description content; (iii) a counterfactual ceiling places perfect delegation 15-31 percentage points above measured performance on every suite, locating large unrealized headroom for future orchestration methods. We release the substrate, annotation layer, reference intervention suite, analysis pipeline, and 220 per-condition run archives.

</details>


### [165] [Progressive Autonomy as Preference Learning: A Formalization of Trust Calibration for Agentic Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19151)
*Changkun Ou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将智能体工具使用中的信任校准问题形式化为偏好学习问题，通过策略网关维护高斯过程后验来预测人类风险容忍度，并在最不确定时升级给人类审批。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能体自主使用工具时，需要在完全自主执行和需要人类审批之间做出恰当决策，即信任校准问题。

Method: 将信任校准建模为偏好学习问题；策略网关使用高斯过程后验（通过probit似然从二元审批反馈中学习）来估计人类风险容忍度；在最不确定的决策点升级给人类；该框架本质上是偏好贝叶斯优化的实例。

Result: 该方法在结构上继承了偏好贝叶斯优化的推理机制（近似高斯过程分类）和样本效率优势（不确定性导向的查询），但目标不同：将动作空间划分为允许/阻止/询问三个区域，而非优化设计。

Conclusion: 信任校准可被视为偏好学习问题，偏好贝叶斯优化框架适用于此场景，但需要调整目标函数从优化变为动作空间分类。

Abstract: We formalize trust calibration for agentic tool use (deciding when an automated agent's proposed action may execute autonomously versus require human approval) as a preference-learning problem. A policy gateway maintains a Gaussian-process posterior over a latent human risk-tolerance function, observed through a probit likelihood on binary approve/deny feedback, and escalates to the human exactly where the approval outcome is most uncertain. We show this is structurally an instance of Preferential Bayesian Optimization, inheriting its inference machinery (approximate Gaussian-process classification) and its sample-efficiency argument (uncertainty-targeted querying), while differing in objective: classifying an action space into allow/block/ask regions rather than optimizing a design.

</details>


### [166] [Hallucination as Exploit: Evidence-Carrying Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19192)
*Guijia Zhang,Hao Zheng,Harry Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出证据携带多模态智能体（ECA），通过将模型文本视为不可采信证据，利用结构化验证器为工具调用提供客观凭证，从而解决多模态智能体因视觉幻觉导致未授权操作的安全问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体依赖截图、文档等视觉信息选择工具调用，当模型产生虚假视觉claim并据此执行点击、邮件、转账等特权操作时，幻觉问题从回答质量错误升级为授权失效的安全漏洞。现有方法缺乏对此类"幻觉到行动转化"故障模式的系统性防御。

Method: ECA将每个工具调用分解为动作关键谓词，通过受限的DOM/OCR/AX验证器获取类型化证书，由确定性门控仅授予证书支持的权限。模型自由文本被视为不可采信证据，不直接用于授权决策。架构将不透明的模型信念转化为可审计的验证器、模式与实现残差。

Result: 在1,900次攻击验证器红队测试中，四轮针对性加固将门控绕过率从15%降至1.3%。200任务端到端管线实现0%不安全行动率（Wilson 95%上界2.67%），120任务浏览器概念验证上界4.3%。500任务HACR审计显示：朴素智能体不安全执行率100.0%，仅提示词防御49.6%，ECA为零。

Conclusion: 模型语言可以提议行动，但外部证据必须授权行动。ECA通过将授权决策与模型内容解耦，从根本上消除了基于幻觉的工具调用安全风险。

Abstract: Multimodal agents use screenshots, documents, and webpages to choose tool calls. When a false visual claim triggers a click, email, extraction, or transfer, hallucination becomes an authorization failure rather than an answer-quality error. We formalize this failure mode as hallucination-to-action conversion: an unsupported perceptual claim supplies the precondition that makes a privileged action appear permitted. We propose evidence-carrying multimodal agents (ECA), which treat free-form model text as inadmissible evidence. ECA decomposes each tool call into action-critical predicates, obtains typed certificates from constrained DOM/OCR/AX verifiers, and lets a deterministic gate grant only the privileges those certificates support. The architecture does not hide perception error; it converts opaque model belief into named verifier, schema, and implementation residuals. Verifier red-teaming over 1,900 attacks exposes this residual directly: four targeted hardening steps reduce gate bypass from 15% to 1.3%. With content-derived certificates, ECA obtains 0% unsafe-action rate on a 200-task end-to-end pipeline (Wilson 95% upper bound 2.67%) and a 120-task browser proof-of-concept (upper bound 4.3%). A direct HACR audit on 500 stratified task keys shows that unsupported action-critical claims reach unsafe execution for naive agents (100.0%) and prompt-only defense (49.6%), but not for ECA. Oracle-certificate replay on 7,488 GPT-5.4 benchmark traces serves as a gate-correctness sanity check, and neural judge baselines remain bypassable under the same threat model. The resulting principle is simple: model language may propose actions, but external evidence must authorize them.

</details>


### [167] [Not all uncertainty is alike: volatility, stochasticity, and exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19215)
*Payam Piray*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出，在具有潜在奖励漂移（波动性）和噪声观测（随机性）的环境中，环境不确定性对探索行为的影响存在非对称性：波动性促进探索，而随机性抑制探索。作者通过扩展Gittins指数框架和高斯状态空间赌博机模型，从理论上证明了这一非对称性，并提出了CAUSE探索奖励方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将环境不确定性视为同质因素，忽视了不同类型不确定性对探索行为的差异化影响。作者旨在区分波动性与随机性这两种不确定性来源，揭示其对最优探索策略的相反作用机制。

Method: 将Gittins指数框架扩展至高斯状态空间赌博机模型（含潜在动态），推导出波动性和随机性对探索偏好的非对称影响；进一步通过控制即推理的方法，提出CAUSE闭式探索奖励方法，并证明其单调性与Gittins指数一致。

Result: 波动性增强探索，随机性抑制探索；CAUSE在异构噪声结构中优于标准探索策略，并改进了Gittins-per-arm策略（其静止最优性不适用于非静止环境）；噪声推理异常会导致探索方向逆转而非仅受损，为精神疾病的计算模型提供启示。

Conclusion: 学习与探索受同一噪声推理非对称性支配，该框架不仅揭示了波动性与随机性对探索的相反作用，还为理解精神疾病中的异常探索行为提供了计算层面的预测。

Abstract: Adaptive decision-making in biological and artificial intelligence requires balancing the exploitation of known outcomes with the exploration of uncertain alternatives. Although prior work suggests that uncertainty generally promotes exploration, it has typically treated distinct sources of environmental uncertainty as equivalent. We consider environments with latent reward states that drift over time (volatility) and are observed through noisy outcomes (stochasticity). Both increase posterior uncertainty, yet we show they drive optimal exploration in opposite directions: volatility enhances it, stochasticity suppresses it. We establish this asymmetry formally by extending the Gittins index framework to Gaussian state-space bandits with latent dynamics. We further derive Cause-Aware Uncertainty-Sensitive Exploration (CAUSE), a closed-form exploration bonus obtained via control-as-inference that inherits the same monotonicities. CAUSE outperforms standard exploration strategies in environments with heterogeneous noise structure, and also improves on a Gittins-per-arm policy whose rested-bandit optimality does not transfer to restless settings. Learning and exploration are governed by the same noise-inference asymmetry, and the framework predicts that pathological noise inference produces \emph{reversed} rather than merely impaired exploration, with implications for computational accounts of psychiatric conditions.

</details>


### [168] [Can Large Language Models Revolutionize Survey Research? Experiments with Disaster Preparedness Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19229)
*Yan Wang,Ziyi Guo,Christopher McCarty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了一个五阶段LLM集成框架，用于应对调查研究中回应率下降、样本偏差、数据缺失和AI欺诈等结构性挑战。以2024年飓风米尔顿佛罗里达居民 preparedness 调查（n=946）为实验平台，提出的A-TLM模型在灾难相关的块状MNAR条件下，RMSE（1.439）优于IPW/MI、MICE+PMM和missForest等经典填补方法，同时实现了接近零的有符号偏差（-0.121），而随机森林填补器偏差最大（-0.631）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调查研究面临回应率下降、样本偏差、高风险受访者数据块状缺失以及AI辅助欺诈填写等结构性挑战，尤其是在灾害情境下数据质量至关重要，亟需对LLM在整个调查工作流中的表现进行系统评估。

Method: 提出五阶段LLM集成框架（问卷设计、样本选择、预测试、缺失数据填补、事后分析），构建PMT约束共现知识图谱，开发了零样本推理、检索增强基线和理论驱动变体等七种LLM配置，其中A-TLM模型以PMT因果结构组织检索并在单次模型调用中整合所有证据。

Result: A-TLM在RMSE（1.439 vs 1.496）和偏差（-0.121 vs -0.631）上均优于经典方法；PMT结构化检索优于非结构化检索（MAE 0.993 vs 1.097）；揭示了聚合偏差接近零但掩盖子群体偏差的问题；检索约束知识图谱聊天机器人表明幻觉可通过架构性拒绝来管理。

Conclusion: LLM在调查工作流中具有潜力，但需进行子群体分层偏差审计作为报告标准；幻觉问题可通过架构设计可控；理论约束的检索机制是提升LLM调查表现的关键。

Abstract: Survey research faces mounting structural challenges: declining response rates, sample bias, block-wise missingness among at-risk respondents, and AI-assisted fraudulent completions in online panels. Large language models (LLMs) have been proposed as a remedy, yet rigorous evaluations across the full survey workflow remain scarce, particularly in disaster contexts where data quality matters most. We present and evaluate a five-stage framework for LLM integration covering questionnaire design, sample selection, pilot testing, missing-data imputation, and post-collection analysis, using the 2024 Hurricane Milton preparedness survey of Florida residents (n=946) as a shared empirical testbed. We introduce a Protection Motivation Theory (PMT)-constrained co-occurrence knowledge graph and develop seven LLM configurations spanning zero-shot inference, retrieval-augmented baselines, and novel theory-informed variants. Our proposed Anchored Marginal Theory-Informed LLM (A-TLM) outperforms all three classical imputation baselines (IPW/MI, MICE+PMM, missForest) on RMSE under disaster-relevant block-wise MNAR conditions (S4 RMSE 1.439 vs. 1.496 for the next-best), while achieving near-zero signed bias (-0.121) where the random-forest imputer produces the largest absolute bias (-0.631). Organizing retrieval around PMT causal structure and integrating all evidence in a single model call outperforms unstructured retrieval and staged sequential inference (MAE 0.993 vs. 1.097 for standard RAG). We document that near-zero aggregate bias can mask opposing subgroup errors and propose subgroup-stratified bias auditing as a reporting standard. A retrieval-constrained knowledge-graph chatbot demonstrates that hallucination is architecturally manageable through grounded refusal.

</details>


### [169] [Causal Evidence for Attention Head Imbalance in Modality Conflict Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19250)
*Jinrui Jiang,Zhangtai Wu,Zhen Wu,Xinyu Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过因果分析揭示多模态大模型中注意力头在模态冲突幻觉中的不对称作用，并提出基于因果干预的幻觉抑制方法MACI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解为何视觉证据在多模态大模型生成过程中无法战胜错误文本前提，从机制层面探究内部组件如何驱动或抵抗这种失败。

Method: 对五个开源MLLM进行头部级因果路径补丁分析，识别出两类因果作用相反的注意力头（驱动头与抵抗头）；提出MACI方法，在检测到模态冲突时条件性地抑制驱动头。

Result: 发现驱动效应分布更广且权重更大，抵抗效应集中在少数高重要性头部；MACI在MMMC基准上实现最大幻觉减少，且幻觉-准确率权衡良好，可零样本迁移至SCI-SemanticConflict测试。

Conclusion: 多模态幻觉源于分布式驱动与局部化抵抗的不平衡路由结构；针对性的因果干预可有效缓解该问题。

Abstract: Modality-conflict hallucination occurs when multimodal large language models (MLLMs) prioritize erroneous textual premises over contradictory visual evidence. To understand why visual evidence fails to prevail during generation, we take a mechanistic perspective and examine which internal components drive or resist this failure. We perform head-level causal analysis using path patching across five open-source MLLMs and identify two groups of attention heads with opposing causal roles: hallucination-driving heads and hallucination-resisting heads. We find a consistent asymmetry: driving effects are more broadly distributed and carry greater aggregate weight, whereas resisting effects concentrate in a small number of high-importance heads. Ablation experiments further confirm that these groups exert opposing effects during generation: distributed driving influence and localized resistance together form an imbalanced routing structure that biases generation toward the erroneous premise. Motivated by this finding, we propose MACI (Modality-conflict-Aware Causal Intervention), a conditional intervention that suppresses causally identified hallucination-driving heads only when conflict is detected. Across five MLLMs, MACI achieves the largest hallucination reduction among compared inference-time baselines on the MMMC benchmark with a favorable hallucination-accuracy trade-off, and transfers zero-shot to the SCI-SemanticConflict test.

</details>


### [170] [AQuaUI: Visual Token Reduction for GUI Agents with Adaptive Quadtrees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19260)
*Yuankai Li,Tinghui Zhu,Ha Min Son,Zhe Zhao,Xin Liu,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AquaUI，一种无需训练的推理时令牌缩减方法，通过自适应四叉树利用GUI截图中非均匀的空间信息密度，在保持性能的同时显著减少视觉令牌数量，提升GUI智能体模型的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LMMs作为GUI智能体模型时，高分辨率截图存在信息密度非均匀的问题（大片区域信息量少且视觉同质，而关键文本和图标需要高保真度），已有方法要么需要额外训练，要么忽略GUI的结构化布局和空间冗余。

Method: 提出AquaUI方法：1) 在每张截图输入上构建自适应四叉树，每个叶子节点保留一个代表性合并令牌；2) 保留令牌的空间位置以保证位置编码一致性；3) 提出条件四叉树算法，利用连续截图间的连续性来增强多步交互中的时间一致性。

Result: 在最新GUI智能体模型上实验，在标准基准测试中一致优于基线方法。在GUI-Owl-1.5-32B-Instruct上，实现13.22%加速、29.52%视觉令牌减少，同时保持99.06%的完整令牌性能。

Conclusion: GUI截图的空冗余可以在不重新训练的情况下在推理时被有效利用，AquaUI实现了更好的精度-效率权衡。

Abstract: Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have recently emerged as promising backbones for GUI-agent models, where high-resolution GUI screenshots are introduced to the prompts at each iteration step. However, these screenshots exhibit highly non-uniform spatial information density: large regions may carry little information and are visually homogeneous, while key text and icons may require high visual fidelity. Existing approaches to this problem either require additional training or rely on attention-based token compression, ignoring the structured layout and spatial redundancy of GUI screenshots. To fill the gap, this paper proposes AquaUI, a training-free inference-time token reduction method for GUI agent models that utilizes the non-uniform information density in screenshots. AQuaUI constructs an adaptive quadtree on each screenshot input and keeps one representative merged token per leaf of the quadtree. AQuaUI preserves the spatial positions of retained tokens throughout the pipeline to ensure that all position-encoding stages remain consistent. To further improve temporal consistency across multi-step GUI interactions, we propose a conditional quadtree algorithm that leverages the continuity between consecutive screenshots within a single request. Specifically, it refines the current quadtree using previous quadtrees as references, helping preserve fine-grained regions across static or mildly shifted GUI states. We implement AQuaUI on state-of-the-art GUI agent models and conduct experiments on standard grounding and navigational benchmarks. AQuaUI consistently shows improved accuracy-efficiency trade-offs over prior baselines. Notably, on GUI-Owl-1.5-32B-Instruct, AQuaUI achieves up to 13.22% speedup and 29.52% fewer visual tokens while retaining 99.06% of full-token performance, suggesting that the spatial redundancy of GUI screenshots can be exploited at inference without retraining.

</details>


### [171] [Swimming with Whales: Analysis of Power Imbalances in Stake-Weighted Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19264)
*Yuzhe Zhang,Manvir Schneider,Qin Wang,Davide Grossi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究PoS区块链中基于权益加权的投票治理范式，通过计算社会选择理论中的Penrose-Banzhaf权力指数，分析了权力失衡现象。理论上证明了权益与权力完全对齐不可实现，但特定条件下可在期望上近似；实证上利用Project Catalyst链上治理数据揭示了当前系统中的权力不平衡程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PoS区块链中权益加权投票存在严重的权力扭曲问题——少数大额持币者可控制决策，因此需要量化分析这种权力失衡的程度。

Method: 采用计算社会选择理论中的Penrose-Banzhaf权力指数作为量化工具，结合理论推导与真实链上治理系统（Project Catalyst）的实证数据分析。

Result: 理论上证明了权益与权力完全对齐的不可实现性，但特定条件下可在期望上近似；实证数据揭示了当前权益加权治理系统中存在的具体权力不平衡现象。

Conclusion: 权益加权投票必然存在权力失衡，完全对齐不可行，但在特定条件下可近似实现；真实系统（如Project Catalyst）中存在显著权力不平衡，需关注治理公平性。

Abstract: Voting methods weighted by stakes are the fundamental governance paradigm in Proof-of-Stake (PoS) blockchains. Such a paradigm is known to be prone to power distortions: a few users possessing large stakes may completely control decision making, even without owning the totality of the stakes. We study this phenomenon through the lens of computational social choice, focusing on the extent of power imbalances in stake-weighted voting when power is quantified using the Penrose-Banzhaf power index. Our work presents both analytical and empirical contributions. Analytically, we demonstrate that while a perfect alignment between power and relative stake ownership is generally unattainable, it can be approximated in expectation under specific conditions. Empirically, using data from a real-world on-chain governance system (Project Catalyst), we provide a more fine-grained understanding of the power imbalances that are likely to occur in current stake-weighted governance systems.

</details>


### [172] [MOCHA: Multi-Objective Chebyshev Annealing for Agent Skill Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19330)
*Md Mehrab Tanjim,Jayakumar Subramanian,Xiang Chen,Branislav Kveton,Subhojyoti Mukherjee,Anlan Zhang,Sungchul Kim,Somdeb Sarkhel,Sunav Choudhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MOCHA方法，利用切比雪夫标量化与指数退火策略，在多约束条件下优化LLM智能体技能，相比现有方法在6个任务上均实现提升，平均正确率提高7.5%，发现两倍多的帕累托最优变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示优化器要么忽略多目标权衡，要么用加权求和方式处理，无法覆盖非凸目标区域的帕累托最优变体。

Method: 采用切比雪夫标量化覆盖完整帕累托前沿（含非凸区域），结合指数退火策略实现从探索到利用的平滑过渡。

Result: 在6个多样化智能体技能任务上，MOCHA在所有任务上均有提升，平均正确率相对提升7.5%（FEVER达14.9%，TheoremQA达10.4%），发现的帕累托最优变体数量为基线方法的两倍以上。

Conclusion: MOCHA有效解决了多约束下LLM智能体技能优化的多目标问题，突破了现有优化器在复杂任务上的零改进困境。

Abstract: LLM agents organize behavior through skills - structured natural-language specifications governing how an agent reasons, retrieves, and responds. Unlike monolithic prompts, skills are multi-field artifacts subject to hard platform constraints: description fields are truncated for routing, instruction bodies are compacted via progressive disclosure, and co-resident skills compete for limited context windows. These constraints make skill optimization inherently multi-objective: a skill must simultaneously maximize task performance and satisfy platform limits. Yet existing prompt optimizers either ignore these trade-offs or collapse them into a weighted sum, missing Pareto-optimal variants in non-convex objective regions. We introduce MOCHA (Multi-Objective Chebyshev Annealing), which replaces single-objective selection with Chebyshev scalarization - covering the full Pareto front, including non-convex regions - combined with exponential annealing that transitions from exploration to exploitation. In our experiments across six diverse agent skills - where all methods share the same multi-objective mutation operator and baselines receive identical per-objective textual feedback - existing optimizers fail to improve the seed skill on 4 of 6 tasks: 1000 rollouts yield zero progress. MOCHA breaks through on every task, achieving 7.5% relative improvement in mean correctness over the strongest baseline (up to 14.9% on FEVER and 10.4% on TheoremQA) while discovering twice as many more Pareto-optimal skill variants.

</details>


### [173] [Generative Recursive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19376)
*Junyeob Baek,Mingyu Jo,Minsu Kim,Mengye Ren,Yoshua Bengio,Sungjin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出生成式递归推理模型（GRAM），将递归潜在推理转化为概率多轨迹计算，支持随机潜在轨迹、多种假设和推理时扩展，在结构化推理和多解约束满足任务上优于确定性基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有递归推理模型（RRMs）大多是确定性的，只能沿着单一潜在轨迹收敛到单一预测，缺乏处理多假设和替代解策略的能力。

Method: GRAM将递归潜在推理建模为随机潜在轨迹，支持通过递归深度和平行轨迹采样进行推理时扩展，形成潜变量生成模型，支持条件推理pθ(y|x)和无条件生成pθ(x)，使用摊销变分推理训练。

Result: 在结构化推理和多解约束满足任务上，GRAM优于确定性递归和循环基线模型，并展示了无条件生成能力。

Conclusion: GRAM通过概率化递归推理实现了多假设探索和推理时扩展，为神经推理系统实现扩展计算提供了有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: How should future neural reasoning systems implement extended computation? Recursive Reasoning Models (RRMs) offer a promising alternative to autoregressive sequence extension by performing iterative latent-state refinement with shared transition functions. Yet existing RRMs are largely deterministic, following a single latent trajectory and converging to a single prediction. We introduce \emph{Generative Recursive reAsoning Models (GRAM)}, a framework that turns recursive latent reasoning into probabilistic multi-trajectory computation. GRAM models reasoning as a stochastic latent trajectory, enabling multiple hypotheses, alternative solution strategies, and inference-time scaling through both recursive depth and parallel trajectory sampling. This yields a latent-variable generative model supporting conditional reasoning via $p_θ(y \mid x)$ and, with fixed or absent inputs, unconditional generation via $p_θ(x)$. Trained with amortized variational inference, GRAM improves over deterministic recurrent and recursive baselines on structured reasoning and multi-solution constraint satisfaction tasks, while demonstrating an unconditional generation capability. \href{https://ahn-ml.github.io/gram-website/}{https://ahn-ml.github.io/gram-website}

</details>


### [174] [What and When to Distill: Selective Hindsight Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19447)
*Xiaozhe Li,Tianyi Lyu,Yang Li,Yichuan Ma,Peiji Li,Linyang Li,Qipeng Guo,Dahua Lin,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SERL框架，通过选择性环境加权强化学习，在长程任务中利用环境反馈（错误信息、页面变化等）替代轨迹级奖励，在ALFWorld和WebShop上分别达到90.0%和80.1%的成功率，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在处理长程稀疏奖励时存在信用分配困难，且未能充分利用多轮交互中每个步骤的环境反馈（如错误信息、页面变化等）。

Method: 提出SERL框架，用任务奖励决定更新方向，环境反馈调整动作的权重和位置，聚焦关键动作；系统研究了5种反馈来源和2种插入粒度。

Result: 在ALFWorld上达90.0%，WebShop上达80.1%的成功率，优于强RL和蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: 有根据的、与动作相关的、在关键节点的环境反馈优于不加区分地使用更长或更丰富的上下文。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning can train LLM agents from sparse task rewards, but long-horizon credit assignment remains challenging: a single success-or-failure signal must be distributed across many actions. Existing methods rely on trajectory-level rewards or proxy signals, without fully leveraging per-step environmental feedback. Multi-turn agent settings are underexplored, where feedback can include error messages, page changes, observations, or reference trajectories. We systematically study five feedback sources and two insertion granularities and introduce SERL, a selective environment-reweighted learning framework. SERL uses the task reward to determine update direction, while environment feedback adjusts placement and magnitude, focusing on critical actions. On ALFWorld and WebShop, SERL achieves 90.0% and 80.1% success, outperforming strong RL and distillation baselines. Analysis shows that grounded, action-relevant feedback at meaningful points consistently outperforms indiscriminate use of longer or richer context.

</details>


### [175] [Beyond Mode Collapse: Distribution Matching for Diverse Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19461)
*Xiaozhe Li,Yang Li,Xinyu Fang,Shengyuan Ding,Peiji Li,Yongkang Chen,Yichuan Ma,Tianyi Lyu,Linyang Li,Dahua Lin,Qipeng Guo,Qingwen Liu,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DMPO通过前向KL散度的近似优化，在on-policy强化学习中有效防止模式坍塌，在NP-hard组合优化、数学推理等任务上持续探索并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等on-policy RL方法存在模式坍塌问题——一旦发现高奖励解，概率质量会集中到该单一解上，停止探索其他策略。作者认为这源于反向KL最小化的mode-seeking行为。

Method: 提出DMPO，通过构造与奖励成比例的组级目标分布，然后对齐策略分布到该目标，实现前向KL最小化的近似。这种方式在不需从全局目标分布采样的前提下，提供mode-covering行为，维持持续探索。

Result: 在NP-Bench（文本版）上达43.9% Quality Ratio（GRPO 40.1%），NP-Bench（视觉版）达43.1%（GRPO 38.4%），相对提升9%和12%；数学推理任务提升+2.0%，域外任务提升+2.3%。

Conclusion: 分布匹配是一种实用且 principled 的防止on-policy RL模式坍塌的方法，通过维持多样性训练提升通用推理能力，在多种推理任务上实现一致的性能改进。

Abstract: On-policy reinforcement learning methods like GRPO suffer from mode collapse: they exhibit reduced solution diversity, concentrating probability mass on a single solution once discovered and ceasing exploration of alternative strategies. We show this stems from reverse KL minimization's mode-seeking behavior, which reinforces the first high-reward trajectory found rather than maintaining a distribution over multiple diverse solutions. We propose DMPO (Distribution-Matching Policy Optimization), which prevents mode collapse through principled approximation of forward KL minimization. DMPO constructs a group level target distribution over sampled trajectories proportional to their rewards, then aligns the policy distribution to this target. This provides mode-covering behavior without requiring sampling from the intractable global target distribution, enabling sustained exploration throughout training. We validate DMPO on NP-hard combinatorial optimization, where exponentially many feasible solutions exist but only a few approach optimality, an ideal testbed for evaluating exploration. DMPO achieves 43.9% Quality Ratio on text-based NP-Bench (vs. GRPO's 40.1%) and 43.1% on vision-based NP-Bench (vs. 38.4%), demonstrating 9% and 12% relative improvements respectively. These gains generalize to mathematical reasoning (+2.0%) and out-of-domain tasks (+2.3%), showing that diversity-preserving training enhances general reasoning capabilities across modalities. Our work establishes distribution matching as a practical, principled approach to preventing mode collapse in on-policy RL, with consistent quality improvements demonstrating sustained exploration across diverse reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [176] [Attention-Guided Reward for Reinforcement Learning-based Jailbreak against Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19485)
*Zheng Lin,Zhenxing Niu,Haoxuan Ji,Yuzhe Huang,Haichang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现大型推理模型（LRM）越狱攻击的成功率与其注意力模式相关，并基于此提出一种强化学习驱动的越狱方法，结合注意力信号作为奖励函数，并引入多样化说服策略，在多个模型和基准测试上取得了更高的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRM虽然推理能力强，但暴露内部推理过程带来了额外的安全风险，现有研究表明LRM比标准LLM更容易受到越狱攻击，因此需要研究其漏洞机理并提出更有效的攻击方法来评估安全性。

Method: 利用强化学习（RL）优化越狱攻击，将注意力信号显式纳入奖励函数设计；同时引入多样化说服策略（persuasion strategies）丰富RL动作空间，以提升攻击成功率。

Result: 在5个开源和闭源LRM、3个基准测试上，该方法在攻击有效性、效率和迁移性方面均显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: LRM的注意力模式与越狱攻击成功率密切相关，基于强化学习和注意力引导的攻击方法能有效突破LRM的安全防护。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in solving complex problems by generating structured, step-by-step reasoning content. However, exposing a model's internal reasoning process introduces additional safety risks; for example, recent studies show that LRMs are more vulnerable to jailbreak attacks than standard LLMs. In this paper, we investigate jailbreak attacks on LRMs and reveal that the attack success rate (ASR) is closely correlated with LRMs' attention patterns. Specifically, successful jailbreaks tend to assign lower attention to harmful tokens in the input prompt, while allocating higher attention to those tokens in the reasoning content. Motivated by this finding, we propose a novel jailbreak method for LRMs that leverages reinforcement learning (RL) to enhance attack effectiveness, explicitly incorporating attention signals into the reward function design. In addition, we introduce diverse persuasion strategies to enrich the RL action space, which consistently improves the ASR. Extensive experiments on five open-source and closed-source LRMs across three benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves substantially higher ASR, outperforming existing approaches in terms of effectiveness, efficiency, and transferability.

</details>


### [177] [Position: The Turing-Completeness of Real-World Autoregressive Transformers Relies Heavily on Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19514)
*Guanyu Cui,Zhewei Wei,Kun He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文区分了两种Transformer图灵完备性证明场景：(i)固定系统设置（更贴近实际LLM部署）和(ii)缩放族设置（现有证明常用）。研究发现，缩放族设置中的证明不构成真正的图灵完备性，而上下文管理方法对实际Transformer的计算能力有决定性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献常声称Transformer是图灵完备的，但混淆了两种不同的设置场景，导致对已有结果的误解。本文旨在澄清这一混淆，并明确实际LLM部署所对应的计算能力。

Method: 首先形式化定义固定系统设置，明确实际LLM的运行方式；然后分析现有证明在缩放族设置下的局限性；最后通过比较不同上下文管理方法的计算能力来论证其关键作用。

Result: 发现现有Transformer图灵完备性证明多基于缩放族设置，该设置虽能提供理论资源界，但不构成真正的图灵完备性；不同上下文管理方法可导致截然不同的计算能力。

Conclusion: 上下文管理是决定实际自回归Transformer计算能力的关键组件，未来研究应更关注固定系统设置下的计算特性。

Abstract: Many works make the eye-catching claim that Transformers are Turing-complete. However, the literature often conflates two distinct settings: (i) a fixed Transformer system setting, in which a fixed autoregressive Transformer is coupled with a fixed context-management method to process inputs of different lengths step by step, and (ii) a scaling-family setting, in which a family of different models (with increasing context-window length or numerical precision) is used to handle different input lengths. Existing proofs of Transformer Turing-completeness are frequently established in setting (ii), whereas real-world LLM deployment and the standard notion of Turing-completeness correspond more naturally to setting (i). In this paper, we first formalize the fixed-system setting, thereby providing a concrete characterization of how real-world LLMs operate. We then argue that results proved in the scaling-family setting provide theoretically meaningful resource bounds but do not establish Turing-completeness, thereby clarifying a common misinterpretation of existing results. Finally, we show that different context-management methods can yield sharply different computational power, and we advocate the position that context management is a central component that critically determines the computational power of real-world autoregressive Transformers.

</details>


### [178] [BLINKG: A Benchmark for LLM-Integrated Knowledge Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19518)
*Carla Castedo,Enrique Iglesias,Manuel Lama,Alberto Bugarin-Diz,Maria-Esther Vidal,David Chaves-Fraga*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BLINKG基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在知识图谱构建中的映射能力。实验发现LLMs在简单场景表现良好，但在复杂场景仍有限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱构建中，将数据源与本体术语对齐需要大量人工工作。现有研究缺乏标准化框架来评估LLMs在建立数据模式与本体概念间对应关系的能力。

Method: 提出BLINKG基准测试，包含基于真实用例的递增复杂度场景，对多种最先进LLMs进行实验评估。

Result: LLMs已展现出有前景的解决方案，但在复杂场景下性能仍然有限。

Conclusion: 该基准可评估LLMs当前能力，并定义了实现（半）自动化LLM驱动KG构建的一系列需求，为该领域开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Generating Knowledge Graphs (KGs) remains one of the most time-consuming and labor-intensive tasks for knowledge engineers, as they need to identify semantic equivalences between input data sources and ontology terms. While declarative solutions (e.g., RML, SPARQL-Anything) have helped to generalize this process, aligning input schema elements with ontology terms still involves intricate transformations and requires considerable manual effort. With the advent of Large Language Models (LLMs), there is growing interest in leveraging their capabilities to assist KG engineers. Although some studies have explored using LLMs to automate KG construction, there is still no standardized framework for assessing how effectively they establish correspondences between data schemes and ontology concepts. Therefore, in this paper, we propose BLINKG, a benchmark designed to evaluate the mapping capabilities of LLMs in constructing KGs from heterogeneous data sources. The benchmark includes a set of scenarios with increasing complexity, based on real-world use cases. We conduct an extensive experimental evaluation of several stateof-the-art LLMs using BLINK and observe that they already offer promising solutions. However, their performance remains limited in complex scenarios. Thanks to this benchmark, we can already assess the current capabilities of LLMs for KG construction. Additionally, we define a set of requirements for achieving (semi)automated (LLM-driven) KG construction, opening new research lines in this area.

</details>


### [179] [Efficient Elicitation of Collective Disagreements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19521)
*Mohamed Ouaguenouni,Felipe Garrido-Lucero,Umberto Grandi,César Hidalgo,Magdalena Tydrichova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个分层框架，通过“复数矩阵”这一新概念，量化选民群体对多个选项的分歧结构，发现许多分歧度量需要至少三个选项的偏好信息（层级3），仅靠两两比较不够，并设计了两种偏好采集协议来估计该矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有调查常用两两比较或完整排序来收集选民偏好，但两两比较无法区分结构性分歧与随机噪声。作者希望找到表达分歧度量所需的最少偏好信息层级。

Method: 提出“复数矩阵”（plurality matrix），记录每个子集S中每个选项排第一的概率；定义分歧度量的“层级”为表达它所需的最小子集大小；分析多种现有分歧度量的层级；设计两种偏好采集协议来估计复数矩阵。

Result: 发现排名方差（rank-variance）和分裂性（divisiveness）等分歧度量位于层级3，证明两两比较（层级2）不足以表达；理论上和实验上都展示了超越层级3的价值。

Conclusion: 复数矩阵是分析群体分歧的有力工具，能够以更少认知负荷获取比两两比较更丰富的结构信息，两种采集协议为实际应用提供了灵活选择。

Abstract: We analyze the structure of the disagreement among a population of voters over a set of alternatives. Surveys typically ask either for pairwise comparisons, simple and intuitive for participants, or full rankings over alternatives, eliciting the entire voters' preferences. Building on the observation that pairwise comparisons cannot distinguish structural disagreement from noise, we propose a stratified framework to identify the minimal aggregated preference information needed to compute a number of disagreement measures from the literature. Specifically, we introduce the plurality matrix, a generalization of pairwise comparisons that records, for every subset $S$ of alternatives, the probability that each $a \in S$ ranks first in $S$. We define the level of a disagreement measure as the smallest subset size needed to express it, showing that many existing notions, including rank-variance and divisiveness, sit at level $3$, proving that pairwise comparisons are not enough. In addition, we demonstrate the interest of going beyond level $3$ both theoretically and experimentally. To make these results actionable, we design two elicitation protocols to estimate the plurality matrix, exploring the trade-off between the number of required participants and the cognitive load requested to each of them.

</details>


### [180] [Generative-Evaluative Agreement: A Necessary Validity Criterion for LLM-Enabled Adaptive Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19529)
*Grandee Lee,Yue Wang,Che Yee Lye,Luke Peh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出生成-评估一致性（GEA）作为LLM自评有效性的新标准，发现在两阶段自适应评估中，模型仅恢复约一半的预期方差（r=0.698），存在系统性正向偏差；语法可验证技能GEA强（r>0.7），但设计级技能接近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当同一个LLM同时生成试题、模拟学生回答并评分时，验证循环是自我指涉的，缺乏独立的外部有效性标准。因此需要一种新的有效性准则来判断LLM评分是否真实反映了其生成功能意图测量的技能水平。

Method: 提出生成-评估一致性（GEA）作为有效性准则，直接测量LLM评分函数能否恢复其生成函数被指示产生的技能水平；在两阶段自适应评估场景中进行实证测量。

Result: 模型恢复约一半的预期方差（r=0.698），存在系统性正向偏差；语法可验证技能的GEA强（r>0.7），而设计级技能GEA接近零；低技能高估导致路由阈值附近分数膨胀。

Conclusion: 粒度化的、按技能分解的评分标准是增强GEA的主要机制，并提出了补充缓解措施。

Abstract: When the same LLM generates assessment items, simulates student responses, and scores them, the validation loop is self-referential. We introduce Generative-Evaluative Agreement (GEA), a validity criterion measuring whether an LLM's scoring function recovers the skill levels its generative function was instructed to produce. In the first direct measurement of GEA on a two-stage adaptive assessment, the model recovers roughly half the intended variance r = 0.698 with systematic positive bias. GEA is strong r > 0.7 for syntactically verifiable skills but near zero for design-level skills, and low-skill overestimation inflates scores near the routing threshold. We argue that granular, skill-decomposed rubrics are the principal proposed mechanism for strengthening GEA and outline complementary mitigations.

</details>


### [181] [Library Drift: Diagnosing and Fixing a Silent Failure Mode in Self-Evolving LLM Skill Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19576)
*Xing Zhang,Yanwei Cui,Guanghui Wang,Ziyuan Li,Wei Qiu,Bing Zhu,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了自进化技能库中的“库漂移”问题（技能无节制累积导致性能停滞），通过可控实验复现了该问题，提出了细粒度诊断方法，并给出了一个轻量级治理方案（结果驱动退休+活跃技能上限+元技能先验），将MBPP+ hard-100的pass@1从0.258提升至0.584。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化技能库存在“库漂移”这一静默失败模式——技能无结果驱动的生命周期管理导致检索退化、误注入和性能停滞。现有评估已证实症状（LLM生成的技能收益+0.0pp vs 人工筛选的+16.2pp），但底层机制尚未被隔离和分析。

Method: (1) 通过消融实验隔离漂移：禁用技能注入（平坦基线+0.002）和强制提前退休（主动伤害-0.019）；(2) 构建追加式证据日志，包含每技能贡献分数、归因判定和路由器参与指标；(3) 提出最小治理方案（结果驱动退休+活跃技能上限+元技能先验），并进行八组消融实验分析各机制的贡献。

Result: 在MBPP+ hard-100上经过100轮迭代，held-out pass@1从0.258基线提升至晚期窗口均值0.584（滚动收益+0.328）。八组消融实验详细分解了各治理机制的负载贡献。

Conclusion: 库漂移是自进化智能体中的一个可复现、可诊断、可修复的问题。本文提供的可复现触发条件、追踪级诊断工具和最小治理配方，为任何自进化智能体系统诊断库漂移提供了具体操作手册。

Abstract: Self-evolving skill libraries face a silent failure mode we term \emph{library drift}: unbounded skill accumulation without outcome-driven lifecycle management causes retrieval degradation, false-positive injections, and performance stagnation. Recent evaluation confirms the symptom--LLM-authored skills deliver +0.0pp gain while human-curated ones deliver +16.2pp (SkillsBench)--yet the underlying mechanism has not been isolated. We provide (1) a reproducible trigger: ablations that isolate drift--one disables skill injection (flat floor, +0.002), one imposes premature retirement (active harm, $-$0.019); (2) trace-level diagnostics: an append-only evidence log with per-skill contribution scores, attribution verdicts, and router engagement metrics that make the failure visible before it reaches end-task scores; and (3) a verified fix: a minimal governance recipe (outcome-driven retirement + bounded active-cap + meta-skill authoring prior) that lifts held-out pass@1 from a 0.258 baseline to a late-window mean of 0.584 (rolling gain $+$0.328) on MBPP+ hard-100 over 100 rounds. Eight ablations decompose which governance mechanisms are load-bearing and which are subsumed, providing a concrete playbook for diagnosing library drift in any self-evolving agent.

</details>


### [182] [SceneCode: Executable World Programs for Editable Indoor Scenes with Articulated Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19587)
*Puyi Wang,Yuhao Wang,Linjie Li,Zhengyuan Yang,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Yangguang Li,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SceneCode 将自然语言提示编译为可执行的代码驱动的室内世界程序，生成具有物理交互性、可编辑关节结构且可加载到仿真器的室内场景，无需依赖静态网格或预置资产库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内场景生成方法仅生成静态网格，依赖人工资产库获取关节信息，限制了物体的交互可控性和新可交互资产的按需生成。

Method: 采用房间级智能体主干将提示转换为结构化房屋布局，通过规划器-设计器-评审器循环生成资产请求；利用五种代码生成策略将请求转化为Blender Python程序，经执行引导的修复与优化循环验证后编译为SDF格式的仿真资产，并通过场景状态注册表实现可追踪的本地化编辑。

Result: 在场景级合成、物体级资产质量、人类评估和下游机器人交互任务中，SceneCode生成的程序化世界在提示忠实度、网格结构清洁度和关节元数据的仿真可加载性上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 可执行的代码化世界程序能够有效提升室内场景生成的提示忠实度，并产出结构更清洁、可直接用于物理仿真的可交互资产，为程序化世界生成开辟了新范式。

Abstract: Indoor scene synthesis underpins embodied AI, robotic manipulation, and simulation-based policy evaluation, where a useful scene must specify not only what the environment looks like, but also how its objects are structured. Existing pipelines, however, typically represent generated content as static meshes and inherit articulation only from curated asset libraries, which limits object-level controllability and prevents new interactable assets from being produced on demand. We address this gap by formulating physically interactable indoor scene synthesis as programmatic world generation, and present SceneCode, a framework that compiles a natural language prompt into an executable, code-driven indoor world rather than a collection of opaque meshes. A room-level agentic backbone first turns the prompt into a structured house layout and emits per-object AssetRequests through a planner--designer--critic loop. Each request is then routed to one of five code-generation strategies and converted into a synthesized part-wise Blender Python programs that are validated through an execution-guided repair-and-refine loop. The resulting programs are compiled into simulation-ready assets, and exported as SDF for physics simulation. A persistent scene-state registry links object requests, executable programs, rendered geometry, and simulation assets, turning scene assembly into a traceable and locally editable world-building process. We evaluate SceneCode across scene-level synthesis, object-level asset quality, human judgment, and downstream robot interaction. Results show that executable world programs improve prompt-faithful indoor scene generation and produce assets with cleaner mesh structure, and simulator-loadable articulation metadata. Project page: https://scene-code.github.io/.

</details>


### [183] [Towards Multi-Model LLM Schedulers: Empirical Insights into Offloading and Preemption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19593)
*Mert Yildiz,Pietro Spadaccino,Alexey Rolich,Francesca Cuomo,Andrea Baiocchi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过实证研究分析了多模型LLM服务在异构硬件上的调度问题，重点揭示了层卸载和抢占对不同模型性能的非线性影响，并提出了未来调度器应关注的关键特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统主要针对单模型优化吞吐量，缺乏对多模型、异构硬件环境下资源分配与调度的研究，尤其是GPU内存受限时需进行CPU-GPU部分卸载和抢占的情况。

Method: 对不同LLM在异构硬件平台上进行实证研究，重点分析层卸载和抢占对解码吞吐量的影响，并测量模型状态重载、KV缓存传输等开销。

Result: 1) 卸载导致解码吞吐量出现强非线性且模型依赖的退化，小模型对GPU驻留减少更敏感；2) 抢占开销主要由模型状态重载主导而非KV缓存传输，且不同模型和平台间差异显著；3) 序列长度和互连带宽会放大数据传输和执行效率问题。

Conclusion: 未来调度器需考虑模型特定的卸载敏感性、工作负载特征以及抢占和数据传输的成本结构，以支持异构多模型工作负载的高效混合CPU-GPU执行。

Abstract: Modern deployments of Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly require serving multiple models with diverse architectures, sizes, and specialization on shared, heterogeneous hardware. This setting introduces new challenges for resource allocation, dispatching, and scheduling, particularly under GPU memory constraints where partial CPU-GPU offloading and preemption become necessary. While existing systems primarily optimize throughput for a single model, comparatively little work addresses multi-model scheduling under these conditions. In this paper, we present an empirical study of how different LLMs behave across hardware platforms, focusing on the performance implications of layer offloading and preemption. We show that offloading leads to strongly non-linear and model-dependent degradation in decode throughput, with smaller models exhibiting sharper sensitivity to reduced GPU residency. We further demonstrate that preemption incurs substantial overhead, largely dominated by model state reload rather than key-value cache transfer, and that this cost varies significantly across models and hardware platforms. Additionally, we highlight the role of sequence length and interconnect bandwidth in amplifying data movement and execution inefficiencies. Based on these findings, we identify a set of key features that future schedulers must consider, including model-specific offloading sensitivity, workload characteristics, and the cost structure of preemption and data transfer. These insights provide guidance for the design of next-generation LLM serving systems capable of efficiently managing heterogeneous, multi-model workloads with hybrid CPU-GPU execution.

</details>


### [184] [EMO-BOOST: Emotion-Augmented Audio-Visual Features for Improved Generalization in Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19630)
*Aritra Marik,Marcel Klemt,Anna Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Emo-Boost框架，通过融合基于情感的高级语义线索与低级伪造检测方法，提升深度伪造检测的跨操作泛化能力，在FakeAVCeleb上AUC提升2.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测方法难以泛化到未见过的伪造类型，因为无法为每种新生成技术收集训练数据。

Method: 提出Emo-Boost多模态框架，融合现成的RGB和音频伪造检测器，以及基于情感线索的EmoForensics检测器。EmoForensics利用视觉和音频情感识别模块，建模音视频情感表征的帧内和跨模态时序一致性。

Result: 在FakeAVCeleb数据集上，跨操作泛化AUC平均提升2.1%。

Conclusion: 高级语义情感线索与低级伪造检测方法捕获互补信号，融合两者可有效提升检测泛化能力。

Abstract: With every advancement in generative AI models, forensics is under increasing pressure. The constant emergence of new generation techniques makes it impossible to collect data for each manipulation to train a deepfake detection model. Thus, generalizing to deepfakes unseen during training is one of the major challenges in current deepfake detection research. To tackle this challenge, we employ high-level semantic cues and argue that these cues can support low-level focused approaches in generalizing to unseen types of manipulations. In this work, we study emotions as a high-level semantic cue. We propose Emo-Boost, a multimodal deepfake detection framework that fuses an off-the-shelf RGB- and acoustic-focused deepfake detector with our emotion-based deepfake detector EmoForensics. EmoForensics utilises vision and audio emotion recognition modules and models intra- and inter-modal temporal consistency in emotion representations from an audio-visual stream. We found that EmoForensics and the low-level focused method capture complementary signals. Consequently, combining both signals in EmoBoost enhances the average cross-manipulation generalization AUC by 2.1% on FakeAVCeleb.

</details>


### [185] [When Tabular Foundation Models Meet Strategic Tabular Data: A Prior Alignment Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19662)
*Xinpeng Lv,Yunxin Mao,Renzhe Xu,Chunyuan Zheng,Yikai Chen,Haoxuan Li,Jinxuan Yang,Kun Kuang,Yuanlong Chen,Mingyang Geng,Wanrong Huang,Shixuan Liu,Shaowu Yang,Wenjing Yang,Zhouchen Lin,Haotian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需重新训练的策略感知推理框架SPN，通过构建策略性上下文示例来对齐PFN预测，有效解决了表格基础模型在策略性数据环境中的分布偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型（基于PFN）在非策略性场景中表现优异，但现实世界中个体为获取有利结果会策略性修改自身特征，导致后部署阶段的数据分布偏移，而预训练的非策略性先验与策略性先验不匹配，产生系统性预测偏差。

Method: 提出Strategic Prior-data Fitted Network (SPN)，一种推理时策略感知框架，通过构造策略性上下文示例来近似后操纵输入，并将PFN预测与诱导的策略性分布对齐，无需重新训练模型。

Result: 在真实和合成表格数据集上的实验表明，SPN在策略性操纵下相比表格基础模型和经典表格方法均一致地提升了鲁棒性和预测性能。

Conclusion: SPN有效弥合了非策略性先验与策略性先验之间的差距，为表格基础模型在策略性决策场景中的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models based on pretrained prior-data fitted networks~(PFNs) have shown strong generalization on diverse tabular tasks, but they are typically designed for \emph{non-strategic} settings where data distributions are independent of deployed classifiers. In many real-world decision scenarios, however, individuals may strategically modify their features after deployment to obtain favorable outcomes, inducing a post-deployment distribution shift. This paper studies whether PFN-style tabular foundation models can generalize to such \emph{strategic} tabular data. We show that strategic manipulation creates a mismatch between the non-strategic prior learned during pretraining and the post-manipulation strategic prior, which leads to systematic prediction bias. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{Strategic Prior-data Fitted Network}~\textit{(SPN)}, an inference-time strategy-aware framework that adapts tabular foundation models to strategic environments without retraining. SPN constructs strategic in-context examples to approximate post-manipulation inputs and aligns PFN predictions with the induced strategic distribution. Experiments on real-world and synthetic tabular datasets show that SPN consistently improves robustness and predictive performance under strategic manipulation compared with both tabular foundation models and classical tabular methods.

</details>


### [186] [Pseudocode-Guided Structured Reasoning for Automating Reliable Inference in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.19663)
*Weicong Ni,Tianbao Jiang,Linlin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PStar框架，通过自适应选择结构化伪代码推理路径，显著减少视觉语言模型的幻觉问题，在POPE和MMStar上分别达到87.1%和68.0%的SOTA表现，甚至超越GPT-4V。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
